### New canonical full-completion requirements

<a id="mo-01"></a>
#### MO-01 · Finish versioned procedures and firm-skill governance

**Original obligation:** R L193–195, L474–476; A L253–268. **Priority:** full-completion P1 unless explicitly gated below; safety/authority prerequisite P0 before activation.

**Current source / gap:** Core already has admin-gated firm-skills list/create/update/enable/remove/restore/test, optimistic version checks, history/audit and current procedure validation. Current selected SDK assets/admission do not establish the six built-in procedures or firm versioned procedure execution. Do not build another skill store. Evidence E3/E12.

**Bounded implementation and owner:** Core meri-firm-skills.service.ts and meri-procedure-runtime-validation.service.ts remain canonical. Bind selected runtime/manifest resolution to exact current procedure record/version/digest and trusted native conversation/client mapping. Preserve six named built-ins briefing, draft, bill_analysis, hearing_prep, coalition_letter, amendment_tracker with purpose, scope, evidence prerequisites, capability/effect policy and completion checks. UI settings consume existing admin routes and history/rollback; “use this process next time” creates a reviewable versioned proposal rather than self-modifying instructions. Small writing profile, if its original off-voice trigger is met, uses governed audience/structure/tone/terms/length/citation/exclusions and ≤5approved exemplars, precedence current instruction > client > firm > defaults.

**Dependencies / non-duplication:** MT-13 all14 quick actions remains separate; ST-16 skill settings; MM governance; MF/MG typed selected-skill UI. Keep current SDK pinning and independent business authorization.

**Acceptance:** All6built-ins resolve exact version; firm create/edit/rollback/disable/version conflict; current role/client/ethical wall revoked before and after resolution; altered manifest or allowedTools rejected. Reload retains actual selected version and review. New procedure content cannot grant tools, effects or memory permissions. History/audit survives restore; no stale cached enabled version executes.

<a id="mo-02"></a>
#### MO-02 · Complete durable Cowork jobs and child goal-revision lineage

**Original obligation:** R L218–241, four flows L284–291; A L262–268. **Priority:** full-completion P1 unless explicitly gated below; safety/authority prerequisite P0 before activation.

**Current source / gap:** Durable lifecycle already supports steer/pause/resume/fork and Temporal orchestration. SDK Agent is explicitly foreground, fixed roles and workspace tools. These facts alone do not establish sustained research/deliverable/audit jobs with durable child assignments, goal revisions and background handoff. This is an integration/contract proof gap, not absence of all steering. Evidence E4/E5.

**Bounded implementation and owner:** Extend existing conversation lifecycle/objective-generation/run/event owners, not a new job runner. Define explicit Start Work/Delegate entry and current revisioned objective, requested deliverables/dependencies/capabilities/shared remaining budget. Map current states to queued/planning/executing/waiting/finalizing/completed/partial/failed/cancel-requested/cancelled. Persist assignment identity and goal revision before child dispatch, result/artifact receipts before acknowledgement/notification; recover through existing checkpoints/fences. Resolve fresh delegated authority on resume. Parent reconciles cited child findings/conflicts and owns final narrative/proposals. Legacy research history gets a current-authority read bridge or explicit migration disposition, not silent disappearance.

**Dependencies / non-duplication:** MR-06/07/08 recovery and current steer owners; MF-10/11/12 views; MM-15/16 output/history; PF-06/24 objective/Planner. Approval-wait uses existing hold/resume, consumes zero model calls, and keeps only independent safe work eligible.

**Acceptance:** Four job kinds; explicit chat fast path vs sustained entry; tab detach/reconnect; steer House-only goal revision and reject old child publication; replacement worker at predispatch/result-before-ACK boundaries; cancel requested vs actual stop and committed effects; narrowed child sources/tools/depth/concurrency; no self-approval, grant widening or memory activation. Prove a waiting job makes no polling model calls. Preserve partial useful output and budget liability without replay.

### Retained deferred and conditional features: completion without silent scope loss

- Browser public-workflow qualification remains MT-14/MF-18; advanced visible browser takeover, authenticated site sessions and desktop/device bridge remain MB-01/02/03. Source-held flags are not completion. Any commercial/credential/security decision is a prerequisite, not a deleted feature.
- Full native Office editing remains MM-20; natural-language immutable revision MB-04; source-event routines/history MB-05. All14 original quick actions remain MT-13; 15versioned assets do not create a fifteenth launcher. Mobile/keyboard/focus/reconnect/full-state UI remains MF-20/21 and current3.4design reconciliation.
- Advanced federal drafting, Florida drafting, new USLM default/structural diff: MO-03 retains each named unsupported operation/jurisdiction with official source/schema, exact fixtures, bounded engine extension and review gate. Initial exact replacement/insertion/deletion is not full advanced scope.
- Writing profile, specialist prompts/roles, GovCon playbooks, OCR/table extraction, RRF/temporal graph, additional connectors and new image/voice/social capabilities retain their original explicit triggers and canonical owners in R L474–493. Planning includes these and their prerequisites; none is falsely marked implemented. A chosen alternative framework/model is not itself a user feature and need not be adopted when the accepted existing owner supplies the behavior.
- Fine-tuning/open weights/TR L/PEFT/GPU serving remains HOLD under the original quality/rights/data-consent/retention/heldout-evaluation decision. The user's request to account for every feature does not itself grant training-data permission, vendor spend, credential access or deployment authority. If the full-product definition now requires activating a historically conditional feature, record that product decision explicitly and execute the bounded owner ticket; do not silently reinterpret HOLD as permanent deletion or as authorization to train.
- Strategy-from-research remains PF-06/24 with MO-07/08canonical effects; adoption/onboarding remains MF-20/ST-16product backlog, not recruitment or500turn technical gate. Database relocation is a separately authorized infrastructure decision. Current narrow assistants and Hermes developer tooling remain until explicit accepted retirement.

### Non-duplication and definition of completion

Existing source fixes and receiver PRs are received once through MX-02. Existing PF/WS/ST/ML projects own their domain/provider work; MO tickets add only Meri contract/integration/acceptance obligations not already covered. No MO ticket authorizes a second scheduler, model loop, memory authority, approval service, registry or document store.

The source matrices preserve A–G, R L00–L11, every subfeature/bullet, T1–18, original rollout S1/Stage2/Stage3 rows, D1–10, historical findings1–26 and conditional register entries. Real task evidence may close equivalent rows without repeating paid runs, but inherited assumptions cannot stand in for current receipts. All required features must be implemented, integrated, enabled and qualified with current live evidence where applicable; source-ready/held/notrun/blocked remain distinct. Full completion cannot be claimed while a required feature, current safety defect, provider comparison decision or required retirement action remains open. Staged narrower releases are valid but explicitly not full completion.

<a id="frontend"></a>
## Frontend routes, controls and lifecycle

**Read-only source audit, 21 October 2026. No repository, code, configuration, PR, deployment, account, runtime, provider or external-agent action was performed.** Source-wired means code exists, not that an ordinary deployment enables it or a provider outcome was verified. Tests named below were inspected or located, not run in this audit.

### 1. Decision-ready findings

1. **The ordinary Meri application is considerably smaller than its visual design catalogue.** Current main mounts **five** ordinary routes: `/meri`, `/meri/actions`, `/meri/files`, `/meri/task-updates`, and the held `/meri/memory` route added by the late PR115 merge. There is no mounted `/kit` route anywhere in the complete `apps/web` tree. The large catalogue is `/meri/fixture/[screen]`, plus fixed home/cited-answer/run-activity fixtures. Browser tests calling this a “kit” do not turn it into a production capability.
2. **Main already contains real chat history, recent conversations, first-chat bootstrap, next-turn reservation, fresh native identity-token issuance, exact-run session renewal, bounded streaming and cursor reconnect, Stop, conversation-local task-create approvals, document inspection/download, and narrow personal-preference persistence.** Do not commission these again from a stale unavailable-resource label or README. Their composition flags and live evidence remain separate gates.
3. **Three concrete approval/lifecycle frontend gaps need correction before activation:** pending decisions are not persisted before dispatch; a paginated approval list can retain already-shown data after an authority refusal; paused/waiting-for-approval observation stops without a decision-driven run reread/restart. The first can lose the UI's exact pending-decision guard on reload, the second can retain stale visible proposals after revoked access, and the third can leave run status stale. None proves a live duplicate execution or backend authorization bypass.
4. **The late PR115 merge already corrected stalled-stream copy and received the all-format artifact access reader plus held owner-memory UI.** These are now MAIN at `24316f9`, not pending implementation. MF-08 is regression/qualification only. Merge is not deployment, flag activation, a memory source-packet release, or provider qualification.
5. **Run lifecycle is a missing frontend consumer, not a missing public export.** Meri main contracts 0.1.10 exports conversation-lifecycle (`packages/contracts/package.json:45–49`, Meri main pin above) with control/checkpoint-inspect. The frontend route allowlist and local client adapter have no control/inspect consumer. Backend composition and replay/readback constraints still apply.
6. **Conversation rename/search/archive/delete, a run inbox, an approval inbox, routines management, full file workspace/edit/share/version history, source/citation drilldown, browser-operation controls, workflow launchers and several preferences screens remain design-only or contract/consumer decisions.** Link every one to a bounded ticket and its canonical backend owner. Do not enable fixture links as a shortcut.
7. **Refresh durability differs by surface.** Durable server history is not the same as an unsent draft, in-memory request journal, local fixture state, approval decision marker or artifact-view history. The plan below explicitly separates them.

#### Source baseline and evidence discipline

| Repository/slice | Pin and status used |
|---|---|
| Frontend current main | `neo-capiro/capiro-frontend@24316f913e6082cf33fb0049c1187dc64097975f`, late final freshness check. Earlier full-tree/source audit was at `4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100`; the complete 103-file delta is reconciled below. |

Fresh direct PR metadata re-read confirmed closed/merged. Its 103-file delta includes source/tests/docs and received all-format artifacts, owner-memory and status-copy changes. |

**Late freshness reconciliation:** The 17-commit, 103-file comparison from the earlier pin to current main contains PR115 source reception and no new unrelated ordinary Meri workflow. Changed routes add `/meri/memory` and six fixed owner-memory API paths; existing conversation/approval controllers discussed in MF-03/04/05 are unchanged. Source links at the earlier main or PR head remain pinned lineage evidence; changed-file claims below use the merged PR implementation. The source merge did not itself deploy or enable held capabilities.

The complete `apps/web` tree was retrieved through connected GitHub reads (3,398 tree entries, nontruncated); all non-CSS Meri feature sources, relevant shell/session/Meri adapters, actual Meri API routes and local client exports were inspected from pinned downloads. The initial broad repository-tree call failed transport; a narrower `apps/web` tree succeeded. No inaccessible content is treated as evidence. Negative route claims are based on this tree plus the explicit route allowlists, not on a text search of one file.

Status vocabulary: **MAIN** = source-wired on main; **PR** = available unmerged implementation; **FLAG** = intentionally gated composition; **CONSUMER** = frontend route/state/handler missing despite some backend support; **CONTRACT** = required public behavior absent or unsettled; **FIXTURE** = synthetic design/test surface; **LIVE?** = deployment/provider/runtime result not verified; **DECISION** = product/authority semantics must be chosen before implementation. Multiple labels can apply to one action.

### 2. Route, navigation and proxy ledger

Every ordinary route below still requires authenticated identity, authorized client selection where applicable, correct service origin and deployment flags. A reachable page containing an unavailable message is not evidence that its workflow is enabled.

#### 2.1 Same-origin proxy and authentication contract

| Family | UI → frontend route → public boundary | Existing guard / missing work |
|---|---|---|
| Opaque session | Session host → `/api/action-followthrough/session` → Meri action-host session issuance. Selector body has only client/conversation/run hints, never actor/tenant/grants. [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-actions/session-bootstrap.ts:5–47`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-actions/session-bootstrap.ts#L5-L47) | Native identity freshly issued server-side, receipt correlated to hints; MF-02 tests, not a replacement session scheme. |
| Conversation run | `/api/conversations/[...path]`: initial-input allocation, input admission, submit/history/cancel/events and GET events/UUID/stream. Explicit closed allowlist: [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/input.ts:17–39`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/input.ts#L17-L39) | Rejects caller bearer, compression, Last-Event-ID, cross-site/origin mismatch and malformed cursors: [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/input.ts:48–87`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/input.ts#L48-L87). No control/checkpoints paths. |
| Durable conversation history | Same frontend catch-all dispatches separate public history proxy for `/api/conversations/list`, `/transcript`, `/next-turn`: [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/history-proxy.ts:40–74`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/history-proxy.ts#L40-L74) | Native Clerk identity; public request/response validation; unknown outcome retained after a dispatched next-turn failure: [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/history-proxy.ts:89–167`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/history-proxy.ts#L89-L167). Distinct conversation-history cursor, not run-event resume sequence. |
| Stream transport | Proxy uses allowlisted headers, redirect rejection, no-store, bounded SSE, abort cleanup: [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/proxy.ts:16–29`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/proxy.ts#L16-L29), [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/proxy.ts:74–124`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/proxy.ts#L74-L124) | 35-second stream window, 8-MiB/timeout/cancellation bound: [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/stream.ts:2–82`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/stream.ts#L2-L82). Transport abort is not Stop. Preserve these controls for new lifecycle adapters. |
| Intents approvals | `/api/conversations/intents/[operation]` → versioned list/decide using session credential; response receiver checks proposal/request correlation. [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-intents/access.ts:71–107`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-intents/access.ts#L71-L107), [`apps/web/src/shared/meri-intents/access.ts:128–157`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-intents/access.ts#L128-L157) | Only current published `task.create` decision capability. Missing denial invalidation/pending persistence/run refresh: MF-03/04/05. |
| Actions | `/api/action-followthrough/[operation]`; session issuance uses fresh native identity, non-session routes use the action adapter's configured token mode. [`apps/web/src/app/api/action-followthrough/[operation]/route.ts:18–27`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/app/api/action-followthrough/[operation]/route.ts#L18-L27) | No browser approval authority. Query/draft/prepare/execute are not interchangeable with conversational intents. MF-12 aligns handoff, does not invent a new execution owner. |
| Task commands | `/api/task-commands/[operation]` → prepare/execute typed task-command adapter. | Fixed public operation selection, server-owned grant. Backend MT qualification precedes enabling. |
| Documents | `/api/documents/[operation]`, `/api/documents/links/[action]`; typed create/revise/inspect and signed-link issue/redeem. | Current main includes PR115 all-format document-access read reception; legacy create/revise stays under its own writer contract. No arbitrary URL fetch/open. MF-14, WS-23/24. |
| Personal preferences | `/api/settings/user/[operation]` → versioned read/write with revision/attempt journal. | Personal setting persistence only. Do not mistake it for policy/model selection, connector OAuth, notifications or account deletion. ST-12–19. |
| Core canonical operations | `/api/capiro/[operation]`; first-chat bootstrap and authorized client reads use Core operation registry, not a raw browser backend URL. | Keep native actor/tenant/client correlation, no duplicated Core records or browser grants. PF/WS/ST/ML remain owners. |

**Configuration must be qualified as a matrix, not a single “Meri enabled” switch.** Auth + issue-free service config + configured Meri base URL are prerequisites; conversation additionally requires HTTPS root origin. Separate flags exist for action workflow, task commands, conversation workflow, document workflow plus fixed document operation ([`apps/web/src/shared/meri-actions/connection-config.ts:8–40`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-actions/connection-config.ts#L8-L40)), first-chat bootstrap plus configured client ([`apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/config.ts:11–25`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-conversations/config.ts#L11-L25)), conversation intents ([`apps/web/src/shared/meri-intents/config.ts:11–14`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-intents/config.ts#L11-L14)) and user preferences ([`apps/web/src/shared/meri-user-preferences/config.ts:13–18`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/shared/meri-user-preferences/config.ts#L13-L18)). Preview restrictions are enforced separately in middleware and route guards; ordinary-environment qualification must not silently bypass them.

### 3. Complete functional action ledger

The ledger is grouped by action family, not by repeated mobile/dark fixtures. Every fixture slug is enumerated in Appendix A and every inspected JSX control site is indexed in the companion CSV. “Missing” below means the visible design is not an ordinary consumer; it does not deny backend implementation described in MR/MT/MM.

#### 3.4 Late-merged owner-memory route and controls

Current main adds six fixed API route shapes: `/api/memory/owner/candidates`, `/api/memory/owner/candidates/[id]/decision`, `/status`, `/version-decision`, `/api/memory/owner/items`, and `/api/memory/owner/items/[id]/corrections`. The existing source-packet, feature and preview guards still decide whether these can forward; this is not a general memory-admin API.

| Control | State and required durable behavior |
|---|---|
| Inbox refresh/retry/page | Real owner inbox consumer added; current owner identity and Core read own pending state. Source packet/flag hold remains ST-11/MM. |
| Choose approve/reject, confirm decision, rejection reason | Two-stage UI, rejection reason required, Core owns decision eligibility. Do not generalize into organization governance. |
| Unknown decision/reload recovery | **Already hardened in this new memory slice:** pre-dispatch hold, identity-scoped storage and fail-closed storage failure. [owner-decision-hold.ts1–68](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/24316f913e6082cf33fb0049c1187dc64097975f/apps/web/src/features/meri/memory-review/owner-decision-hold.ts#L1-L68). MF-04 defect concerns the separate conversation task-create intents hook, not this memory hook. |
| Owner item read / correction compose / submit / status | Current-main consumer exists; exact Core updatedAt concurrency token and pending correction semantics remain ST-11/MM qualification. Do not claim active memory changed merely because a correction was submitted. |
| Current wall/owner changes / denied pages | Re-read current authorization, clear inaccessible rows; use merged owner pagination tests as existing coverage, not proof of live qualification. |

[Current route](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/24316f913e6082cf33fb0049c1187dc64097975f/apps/web/src/app/meri/memory/page.tsx#L6-L14); [inbox controls](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/24316f913e6082cf33fb0049c1187dc64097975f/apps/web/src/features/meri/memory-review/MemoryOwnerInbox.tsx#L22-L125); [confirm/reject reason](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/24316f913e6082cf33fb0049c1187dc64097975f/apps/web/src/features/meri/memory-review/MemoryOwnerInbox.tsx#L196-L265).

### 4. Implementation-ready MF tickets

P0 means release/activation blocker for a promised surface, not a claim of an observed production incident. P1 closes correctness or an explicitly designed core workflow. P2 is completeness/product-selection work. A held feature remains held until both its frontend ticket and its owning backend/canonical ticket pass. All proposed writes below are future implementation work, not actions performed by this audit.

<a id="mf-01"></a>
#### MF-01 — Publish truthful capability and route state (P0 activation gate)

- **Root cause / evidence:** Route tree and explicit allowlists disagree with stale `shared/meri-resources/unavailable.ts`: history/create/approvals/preferences now have real slices; lifecycle is publicly exported upstream. Fixture navigation resembles working product.
- **Bounded files/owner:** Frontend shell `SidebarDestinations`, `Sidebar`, `MeriMobileNavigation`, `MeriHome`; `shared/meri-resources/unavailable*.ts`; route-activation tests. Do not edit every screen indiscriminately.
- **Implementation:** Create a reviewed capability table with source pin, ordinary route, deployment flag, accepted public contract version, runtime owner, required receipt and unavailable reason. Render only supported ordinary links; clearly label design fixtures. Replace stale no-contract copy with precise held/not-configured/missing-consumer language. Record PR115 as already merged and preserve its status-copy regression. Distinguish source reception from deployment/flag activation in release notes.
- **Dependencies / exclusions:** MR/MT/MM truth inventory, PR115 merge provenance, canonical PF-01 deployment qualification. No global enable flag or fixture-to-production rewrite.
- **Acceptance:** For every flag/config combination, sidebar/home/mobile/route guard/API agree; no dead href or disabled action claims completed work. Tree test proves actual mounts; `/kit` is not advertised. All fixture destinations remain recognizably non-authoritative.

<a id="mf-02"></a>
#### MF-02 — Qualify identity, exact-run renewal and access-loss isolation (P0 before live activation)

- **Root cause / evidence:** Fresh token issuance and 45-second renewal already exist. Source presence does not prove backend bearer lifetime, account mapping, deployment origin or revocation behavior.
- **Bounded files/owner:** `identity-token.server.ts`, `opaque-session-controller.ts`, `session-bootstrap.ts`, `host-controller.ts`, `useConversationConnection.ts`, `SelectedClient.tsx`, shared query scope and their tests. Fix only demonstrated gaps.
- **Implementation:** Build composed acceptance scenarios for verified native actor, tenant/client switch, archived/deleted client, signout, revoked membership, token expiry while streaming and while mutation pending, multiple renewal cycles, browser back/forward, StrictMode/remount. On exact-lifetime loss abort readers, hide actor/client-bound data and invalidate sensitive cached views without auto-canceling backend work. Reissue using fresh identity for exact existing selectors; never allocate next turn on reconnect.
- **Dependencies / exclusions:** MR session-owner expiry/error taxonomy and environment operator qualification. No browser tenant/actor override, persistent bearer storage, weakened issuer/audience or synthetic client.
- **Acceptance:** At least two real renewal cycles preserve run ID and accepted cursor, no duplicate submit/next-turn; stale account/client DOM is hidden immediately, denied data not resurrected by cached pages; explicit retry cannot mint broader authority; native subject-to-actor mismatch fails closed.

<a id="mf-03"></a>
#### MF-03 — Clear approval content on authority refusal (P0 before approvals activation)

- **Root cause / evidence:** Paging refusal currently retains existing rows at [`apps/web/src/features/meri/conversation/intents/useIntentList.ts:55–64`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/features/meri/conversation/intents/useIntentList.ts#L55-L64); access adapter classifies errors but has no host access-loss callback.
- **Bounded files/owner:** `conversation/intents/useIntentList.ts`, `useConversationIntents.ts`, `shared/meri-intents/access.ts`, relevant host lifetime boundary; new unit/browser regressions.
- **Implementation:** Classify published authority denials separately from transient unavailable errors. On ACCESS_DENIED/MEMBERSHIP_REVOKED/MEMBERSHIP_CHANGED or expired/revoked session as contract requires, clear sensitive rows, pending confirmation details and related derived caches; signal parent to retire exact session where appropriate. Preserve harmless pagination continuity only for retryable transport/service outages, visibly explain the distinction. Ignore stale completion from prior identity.
- **Dependencies / exclusions:** Use current intents error vocabulary; no blanket error suppression, no local authorization inference, no new backend grants.
- **Acceptance:** Load first page, revoke access, Load More → zero old proposal content/actions; same for refresh/decision denial and identity switch. Transient page failure keeps admitted rows only under still-valid authority and exposes retry. Out-of-order successful old reply cannot restore data.

<a id="mf-04"></a>
#### MF-04 — Make approval decisions reload-safe and expiry-aware (P1 before approvals activation)

- **Root cause / evidence:** Only uncertain markers are stored; pending state dispatches before persistence and unmounted results are dropped ([`apps/web/src/features/meri/conversation/intents/useConversationIntents.ts:25–64`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/features/meri/conversation/intents/useConversationIntents.ts#L25-L64), [`apps/web/src/features/meri/conversation/intents/useConversationIntents.ts:150–180`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/features/meri/conversation/intents/useConversationIntents.ts#L150-L180)). Clock expiry has no refresh timer.
- **Bounded files/owner:** `useConversationIntents.ts`, `intent-view.ts`, `ConversationIntents.tsx`; bounded per-tab decision journal helper and tests.
- **Implementation:** Before dispatch, synchronously retain an immutable non-secret decision identity: tenant/actor/client/conversation, delivery/proposal/hash/choice and attempt phase/version. Restore pending as unknown, reconcile durable list first, permit only exact same decision request while unresolved. Handle blocked storage explicitly or use an approved durable recovery owner; do not silently claim reload safety. Clear resolved markers only after correlated durable evidence. Recompute expiry at deadline/visibility return and after each refresh.
- **Dependencies / exclusions:** Published list/decide idempotency remains server authority. Retention/privacy decision for journal; no credential or full sensitive proposal persistence. MR/MT receipt semantics if list cannot resolve a state.
- **Acceptance:** Reload/unmount immediately after dispatch, dropped response, two tabs, expired proposal, changed hash/reviewer and storage failure cannot produce a contradictory replacement decision or false success. Exact replay is accepted once by backend; settled receipt clears journal; expiry disables action without relying on another click.

<a id="mf-05"></a>
#### MF-05 — Resume run observation after approval or external state change (P1)

- **Root cause / evidence:** Paused/waiting-for-approval is treated settled; decisions only `await refresh()` intents ([`apps/web/src/features/meri/conversation/intents/useConversationIntents.ts:180–182`](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-frontend/blob/4f9a7e8dcaa9ba03494dc5a49559daf70cea5100/apps/web/src/features/meri/conversation/intents/useConversationIntents.ts#L180-L182)), while run observation starts once per mounted port.
- **Bounded files/owner:** `ConversationPanels.tsx`, `useConversationIntents.ts`, `useLiveRun.ts`, `useRunObservation.ts`, `conversation-view.ts`, `RunStatus.tsx`.
- **Implementation:** Expose a narrow decision-settled/status-may-have-changed callback that requests authoritative run history then resumes event observation only if current status warrants. Add explicit Refresh run status for paused/waiting/unknown; bounded visibility/focus refresh or server event mechanism for external approver. Separate terminal from temporarily quiescent state. Do not resubmit prompt, infer resumed status, or repeatedly poll an expired session.
- **Dependencies / exclusions:** Runtime lifecycle/approval continuation gates and fresh target-session requirements; MF-02/03/04.
- **Acceptance:** Approve same tab and another reviewer tab: current run eventually shows real resumed/completed/refused state without navigation or token renewal. No duplicated submit/decision; denied refresh clears stale data; paused remains paused until authority says otherwise; no busy infinite loop.

<a id="mf-06"></a>
#### MF-06 — Expose Stop retirement and next-turn readiness honestly (P1)

- **Root cause / evidence:** Stop fence acknowledgement precedes physical retirement; next-turn can correctly conflict. Frontend retry window is150s, whereas backend cleanup can outlast it. Existing history/new-chat/next-turn consumers must be preserved.
- **Bounded files/owner:** `RunStatus.tsx`, `ConversationHistoryStatus.tsx`, `useConversationSession.ts`, `turn-journal.ts`, first-chat/bootstrap/next-turn integration tests.
- **Implementation:** Display separate Stop requested, fence recorded, run terminal and next-turn readiness/cleanup pending where public evidence supports them. Exhausted turn-conflict retry becomes retryable not-ready, not a new conversation/run request. Reconcile same source reservation after reconnect; keep original intent/request identity. For accepted-but-not-started input show recover/readback action rather than a new Send. Avoid unbounded spinner and explain cancellation receipt versus cleanup.
- **Dependencies / exclusions:** MR retirement/current-render fix and readiness/readback semantics. Do not extend frontend timers as substitute for backend recovery or reinterpret409 as permission to allocate.
- **Acceptance:** Stop during tool/provider work, dropped cancel receipt, cleanup delayed beyond150s, fresh deployment and repeated next-turn click all converge to at most one successor; no overlap before retirement; approved prompt preserved and no claim of physical stop without evidence.

<a id="mf-07"></a>
#### MF-07 — Design durable drafts and request recovery across reload (P1)

- **Root cause / evidence:** Unsent drafts are React state; QueryClient journals preserve SPA navigation only. `turn-journal.ts` explicitly warns hard reload loses intent identity; clearing a next-turn composer before successful reservation can lose visible text on reload.
- **Bounded files/owner:** `useConversationSession.ts`, `MeriLiveConversation.tsx`, `MeriComposer.tsx`, `query-journal.ts`, `turn-journal.ts`, first-chat intent helper; a small versioned per-scope journal, not broad local persistence.
- **Implementation:** Define retention for unsent drafts separately from dispatched immutable requests. Save permissible draft text per tenant/actor/client/conversation (or explicitly state drafts not saved); restore only to exact scope and clear on signout/retention expiry. Persist minimal non-secret request identity before mutation if permitted, then use server readback/reservation on reload. Unknown submission must not create new logical request. Distinguish edited newer draft from acknowledged text and preserve IME/blocked-enter behavior.
- **Dependencies / exclusions:** Privacy/retention ST-18; MR durable admission/readback owner; MF-04 journal conventions. Never persist bearer/session credentials or trust restored bytes as authority.
- **Acceptance:** Reload before dispatch, after dispatch/before ACK, after ACK, during next-turn409, offline, storage-full and account/client switch: no accidental resend, no cross-scope draft, no unexplained loss of accepted request. Hard-reload outcome recovered from durable server state, not guessed from local cache.

Keep observing/reconnecting/stalled/outcome-unknown separate; page departure stops watching, not execution. Audit auxiliary workflow errors for same optimistic wording.

Publish owner-scoped paginated searchable summary and revision/idempotency-protected mutations. Then mount real history/search/palette and management actions with loading/empty/denied/error/uncertain/reload states. Preserve conversation/client/run routing and clear removed records from all lists.

Present source/target lineage and unknown-outcome readback; never call private lifecycle APIs or automatically replay as new request.

### 5. Cross-owner mapping and sequencing

| Canonical owner | Meri frontend work here, without duplication |
|---|---|
| PF-02/03 | MF-19 consumes Ask Meri/client profile/Create with Meri context; Portfolio owns starter placement and object semantics. |
| WS-22 | MF-14/19 consume general generation/agent results; no replacement Workspace generation service. |
| WS-23 | PR115 all-format source is already merged; MF-14 supplies Meri consumer acceptance, not duplicate vendoring. |
| WS-24 | MF-14/15 are Meri-specific library/detail/upload/preview/history/filing consumer slices under same canonical custody/version/permission owner. |
| ST-07–11 | MF-13/17 reflect memory governance/grants/files/notifications/owner-memory, not new governance model. |
| ST-12–19 | MF-17/18/20 attach persistence/consumption, defaults, connectors, usage, skills, updates, privacy and help acceptance; original tickets remain canonical. |
| ST-28 | MF-02/19 exact client-context rebind. |
| ML-05/06 | MF-19 does not implement fake reply or new provider send owner; preserve canonical connection/sender/thread. |
| MR | Sessions, durable run lifecycle/retirement/next-turn/readback, routine owner/firing, runtime browser/continuation. MF implements consumers and honest UI state only. |
| MT | Real tool registry, task-create/update execution/grants, mail/read scopes and capability truth. No client authority expansion. |
| MM | Upload/read attachments, artifact writer/read/renderer/custody, memory/research receipts. No browser substitute for backend provenance. |

**Sequence:** (1) MF01/02/03/04/08/21 and qualify the already-received PR115 source, with flags held; (2) MF05/06/07 correctness and composed chat/approval qualification; (3) MF10/12/14/16/19 existing-contract consumers and canonical handoffs; (4) publish missing management/index/inbox/read packets then MF09/11/13/15; (5) deliberate MF17/18 product scope and MF20 across every shipped slice. Independent safe read-only consumers may run in parallel, but a UI ticket does not waive a runtime/migration/authority gate.

### 6. Existing tests, mandatory new cases and unknowns

Existing source tree contains dedicated tests for action adapter/bootstrap/correlation/host/lifetime/proxy/session/transport; client selector; conversation host/live/proxy/reconnect/route; document adapter/bounds/journal/links/proxy/transport; files download/draft/state/workbook; first-chat Core/host/preview-policy/route; history not-started/proxy; identity-token; initial draft/input proxy/recovery; intents access/gating/proxy/view; preview-route activation; task-command adapter/proxy; turn settlement; unavailable resources; preferences proxy/route/host. Browser suites cover host lifetime/navigation/mobile/workflow, conversation artifacts/host/selection, files lifetime/navigation/workflow, history, initial input/reconnect, intents dark, new conversation, production data, stored-conversation recovery and task-command workflow. These are existing test **sources**, not fresh pass results.

Add explicit regression cases for the three newly isolated defects: approval Load More after membership denial, unload immediately after decision dispatch, and waiting-for-approval→running after same-tab or external decision. Add hard-reload unknown-input/next-turn identity, Stop cleanup beyond frontend retry window, two renewal cycles with current native identity, stale successful responses after identity switch, denied file URL after lifetime abort, live source/citation denial and exact-version artifact deep links. Preserve clean-empty SSE window behavior, sequence duplicate/gap rejection, immutable same-request retries, no broad bearer forwarding, no redirect/compression acceptance and signed-byte verification.

Unknowns requiring implementation-stage qualification, not guesses:
- Actual deployment flags, ordinary versus restricted preview exposure, current upstream origin and native identity configuration.
- Live membership/actor mapping, migrations, runtime worker ports, continuation candidate, routine management owner and effective provider/tool grant.
- Whether queued/paused/approval transitions emit enough current public events for UI-only reconciliation; use explicit history read until proven.
- Product policy for conversation naming, archive/delete recovery, unsent draft retention, full document editing/comments/sharing, run/approval inbox scope and supported templates/model selection.
- Contract and owner for typed source details, durable file/run indexes, request-status readback where current list/history is insufficient.
- Provider-qualified task update/mail/artifact results and exact composed renewal/retirement behavior. This audit deliberately did not perform them.
- Current CI/deployment pass status after the pinned metadata reads. Old PR descriptions and fixture success counts are not substituted.

No denied operation was bypassed. No source test or provider action was executed. The output is a plan, not a claim that any listed defect was fixed.

### Appendix B. Control-site and test-source review index

The companion `frontend-control-sites.csv` contains **659 JSX control/handler/disabled/href source sites** across the pinned Meri feature sources. It is an exhaustive lexical navigation aid, not a claim that every site is a separate action or enabled capability. The semantic action ledger above is authoritative about routing, durability and missing owners. New current-main owner-memory controls are separately enumerated in §3.4 and are not mislabeled as the old fixture-only memory catalogue.

All implementation tickets have bounded files, a public-contract/owner dependency and a refresh-based acceptance condition. No item is considered closed merely because a button gains an onClick handler, a mock responds, a fixture looks complete, a contract type exists or a flag can be set.

## Runtime, continuity, routines and recovery

> **Final source freshness correction,17:50UTC:** Meri main remains bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996. PR197 now ef3fbbd964ff4ff86cc12026458fce2c88a7d787 includes that main (+33/-0), so old receiver-conflict comments are historical, not an open merge task. P2 PR196 remains c703e9f3 and separately owned. Frontend115 is now merged; the runtime findings below remain pinned to unchanged Meri main.

# Meri runtime, session and continuity closeout audit
### Read-only finding and implementation plan, 21 October 2026

#### Executive finding

Meri is not missing its basic runtime. Current main contains initial admission, one-query SDK execution, durable UI events, Stop fencing, retirement reconciliation, session mirroring, completion checkpoints, later-turn reservation, lifecycle controls, routine firing and receipt-first accounting. The user's successful Fable first chat on 20 October remains a valid reported win.

The most important newly identified mismatch is narrower and consequential: a restarted **dynamic first-turn original** is still checked against the current deployment configuration on its stop-only retirement path. Candidate11 proves a conversation can continue under a new image *after* the original is retired. It does not prove the new image can retire an old, still-owned original. The cleanup path can reject before reaching physical stop. This is source-confirmed; a live stranded-task incident is **not** established.

Other concrete closure work concerns worker control-read error classification, foreground routines' dependence on an active execution allocation, routine-consent receipt repair, and production retention/purge composition. The remaining lifecycle, SDK, accounting and routine tickets below explicitly distinguish missing closure from source-present functionality needing integrated qualification.

#### Scope, pins and evidence discipline

- Reviewed source: `neo-capiro/meri-greenfield@bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996`, verified main through connected GitHub at approximately 17:30 UTC on 21 October 2026.
- Existing P2 PR196 remains unmerged/draft at `c703e9f3a5bde9d3fc41e62d22ce8ac1dda2ff56`. Its focused result report is not a green broad, Docker/image, security or live gate. Parent verified it is +2/-10 against current main; no reported workflow/status entries is not success evidence.
- PR195 telemetry is merged. External P1 owns the one nested operation-budget table. P2 owns owner/proxy pair/replacement security, image and live gates. Neither is recreated by this plan.
- PR178 config adoption is a separate decision. PR197 supplement at `cf634d4f70376c12ee6dd7b97ec22981dbd4cb2a` is not current main and is not the P2 or C10 lane.
- No repository changes, repository code/test/build execution, deployments, provider calls, PR edits or external communications were performed. Source and test files were read through connected GitHub; local commands only inspected scratch source copies and prepared this report. Test descriptions below mean inspected test intent, **not a newly observed pass**.
- Classification: **S** = source-confirmed mismatch/missing composition; **Q** = source-present but release acceptance missing; **D** = explicit capability/policy decision; **U** = consequence or deployment state remains unknown.
- Source checks are not proof of the exact currently deployed Fable image, rendered configuration, Core behavior, Temporal history, database migrations or account/provider settings. The release coordinator owns the deployment/evidence manifest.

### 1. What is already wired, and must not be rewritten

1. Control assembles opaque action sessions, PostgreSQL conversation state, Temporal dispatch, input admission, lifecycle service, native worker boundary and product applications. [apps/runtime/control.mjs:99–122](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/control.mjs#L99-L122)
2. Session renewal is not absent. The backend uses process-local opaque sessions, validates current identity/context on operations and retains the original native bearer per issued session. The FE auditor confirmed fresh Clerk token issuance, bounded renewal, exact same-run hints and cursor-preserving reconnect. This is an integrated acceptance gate, not a request to build renewal again. [apps/control/action-host/session/owner.mjs:62–74](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/action-host/session/owner.mjs#L62-L74), [apps/control/action-host/session/owner.mjs:119–139](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/action-host/session/owner.mjs#L119-L139), [apps/control/action-host/session/owner.mjs:179–191](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/action-host/session/owner.mjs#L179-L191)
3. Native calls already distinguish marked authority nonanswers from definitive refusals. Only load and fixed-event-ID publication are replayable, bounded by the caller's original absolute deadline. [apps/runtime/native-client.mjs:21–36](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/native-client.mjs#L21-L36), [apps/runtime/native-client.mjs:65–82](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/native-client.mjs#L65-L82)
4. Stop returns a durable cancellation fence, not proof that ECS stopped. It independently attempts Temporal cancellation and native original retirement even for an already-terminal run; cleanup survives caller disconnect after authorization. Next-turn admission remains blocked until retirement plus grant revocation, or durable foreclosure proving no allocation authority. [apps/control/conversations/lifecycle/service.mjs:126–155](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/conversations/lifecycle/service.mjs#L126-L155), [apps/control/conversations/next-run.mjs:11–39](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/conversations/next-run.mjs#L11-L39)
5. Recovery does not retry the model attempt. Temporal schedules only bounded, noncancellable reconciliation after failure. After exhaustion a later Stop or the existing one-shot operator reconciler can resume settlement. [apps/control/conversations/recovery-workflow.ts:1–19](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/conversations/recovery-workflow.ts#L1-L19), [apps/runtime/reconcile-run.mjs:1–18](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/reconcile-run.mjs#L1-L18)
6. Completed turns have durable one-shot capture identities. Completion publication atomically records checkpoint verification and completed events; an ACK-loss readback can recover PUBLISHED but does not recapture. [apps/runtime/native-authority/completion-capture.mjs:26–74](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/native-authority/completion-capture.mjs#L26-L74)
7. Candidate11 later turns use the latest published completed checkpoint, including after an intervening failed/cancelled turn. If none exists, a failed/cancelled first turn permits a fresh SDK session only after source settlement. A terminal exact-run session is read-only and does not reserve a successor; explicit next-turn request identity replays its original reservation. [apps/control/conversations/next-run.mjs:1–3](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/conversations/next-run.mjs#L1-L3), [apps/control/conversations/next-run.mjs:25–59](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/conversations/next-run.mjs#L25-L59)
8. Image-only later-turn continuity and predeploy pending successor acceptance exist in source. SDK/CLI mismatch and dormant original configuration drift deliberately refuse. Those are not bugs to solve by relaxing admission. [apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs:53–104](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs#L53-L104)
9. Public lifecycle exports exist. Actual contracts package is 0.1.10 and exports lifecycle, history, input and initial-input. Lifecycle README's old “public export is separate” statement is stale. [packages/contracts/package.json:1–78](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/packages/contracts/package.json#L1-L78)
10. Public lifecycle routes are POST `/api/conversations/control` and POST `/api/conversations/checkpoints/inspect`. Steer, pause, approval.wait, resume and fork use durable controls; fork preserves the source, resume requires retained checkpoint and retired source. Continuation ports are gated by composed intents/current authority and native candidate; fork additionally needs dynamic initial-run owners. [packages/contracts/conversation-lifecycle/protocol.mjs:1–4](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/packages/contracts/conversation-lifecycle/protocol.mjs#L1-L4), [apps/runtime/product-ports/lifecycle.mjs:59–89](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/product-ports/lifecycle.mjs#L59-L89)
11. Routines are not merely fixture screens on the backend: attended owners, Core firing-grant verification, deterministic occurrences, explicit native admission/retirement, overlap barriers and scheduled inbox currentness are implemented. Their live service and finished-chat management behavior still need closure. [apps/runtime/schedule-service-owners.mjs:1–12](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/schedule-service-owners.mjs#L1-L12), [apps/control/schedules/scheduled-occurrence.mjs:27–40](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/scheduled-occurrence.mjs#L27-L40), [apps/control/schedules/scheduled-dispatch.mjs:177–217](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/scheduled-dispatch.mjs#L177-L217)
12. Native model accounting retains UNKNOWN as liability. Terminal usage settlement and readOutcome exist; “no response” is not “no dispatch” and must not refund or resend inference. Provider support is deliberately narrow rather than a general Anthropic passthrough. [apps/gateway/budget/product-model-settlement.mjs:89–138](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/gateway/budget/product-model-settlement.mjs#L89-L138), [apps/proxy/product/response_settlement.py:195–208](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/proxy/product/response_settlement.py#L195-L208)

### 2. Detailed implementation tickets

<a id="mr-01"></a>
#### MR-01 · P0 · Repair historical stop-only retirement after a control render change
**Class:** S, with live consequence U. **Owner:** runtime/native lifecycle. **Release rule:** do not declare cross-render in-flight cleanup qualified until this is closed.

**Exact mismatch**
- A restarted dynamic original has no cached controller. The stop route calls `initial(binding, life, true)`. “true” allows expiry only; `admittedRecord` still defaults to `pinConfiguration=true`. A different configuration SHA rejects before the retirement port. [apps/runtime/prepared-run-registry.mjs:37–46](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/prepared-run-registry.mjs#L37-L46), [apps/runtime/prepared-run-registry.mjs:138–147](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/prepared-run-registry.mjs#L138-L147)
- The retirement port independently calls `initialRecord(..., cleanup=true)`, which still compares current policy, runtime versions, configuration SHA, run window and current schedule bounds. [apps/runtime/prepared-run-bindings.mjs:38–44](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/prepared-run-bindings.mjs#L38-L44), [apps/runtime/prepared-run-bindings.mjs:97–104](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/prepared-run-bindings.mjs#L97-L104)
- It reconstructs issuance facts using the current operations, approved reads, control capability digest and transport; reconstructs allocator task request, qualification, allocation and container from current configuration; then demands hashes equal the original immutable records. Rotation of a credential omitted from the configuration identity can therefore reach a later facts-drift refusal even when the configuration digest is unchanged. [apps/runtime/prepared-run-bindings.mjs:105–111](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/prepared-run-bindings.mjs#L105-L111), [apps/runtime/prepared-run-configuration.mjs:7–16](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/prepared-run-configuration.mjs#L7-L16)
- Historical allocation retrieval exists only for an already RETIRED source. It cannot retire an old live source. [apps/runtime/prepared-run-bindings.mjs:55–68](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/prepared-run-bindings.mjs#L55-L68)

**Why existing tests do not close it**
Candidate11's helper retires the original before render B is constructed. Its deployment tests therefore establish *post-retirement continuation*, not cross-render cleanup. The restart-retirement fixture recreates processes with the same configuration. [apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs:22–45](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs#L22-L45), [apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs:53–63](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs#L53-L63), [apps/runtime/test/restart-retirement.test.mjs:1–70](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/test/restart-retirement.test.mjs#L1-L70)

**Bounded work**
1. Add a stop-only trusted historical-facts resolver over immutable initial-admission, run, issuance and allocation rows. First inventory whether every nonsecret original fact is recoverable; if not, add narrowly scoped immutable custody for future issuances plus a fail-closed legacy repair procedure. Do not assume the facts hash can reconstruct missing facts.
2. Route only authenticated original retirement through that resolver. Match tenant/actor/conversation/full binding, original execution identity, original task ARN/task definition, grant set, hashes and stored request before invoking the existing canonical stop.
3. Separate “valid historical ownership for cleanup” from “eligible under the current render for new execution.” Keep current-render preparation, kickoff, lease, policy, scope, SDK and admission checks untouched.
4. Do not reopen model credentials, mint grants, allocate, restore, run the SDK, or reissue a turn in this path. Preserve ambiguous physical stop and revocation as UNKNOWN. Do not accept caller-supplied historical configuration as authority.
5. Extend `prepared-run-registry.mjs`, `prepared-run-bindings.mjs`, the immutable issuance reader only as necessary, and `restart-retirement.test.mjs`/`conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs`. Reuse `createOriginalRetirement`, not a second allocator. [apps/runtime/native-authority/remote-composition.mjs:98–110](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/native-authority/remote-composition.mjs#L98-L110)

**Dependencies:** current receipt schema, original allocation/issuance owner, release-coordinator migration/release review; P2 only for its existing currentness/security verification.
**Acceptance:** the matrix in §3; every successful receipt must be backed by exact physical-stop evidence and revoked grants, with zero new grants/allocations/model invocations. A successor is still refused until source settlement. No observed live failure is asserted by this audit.

<a id="mr-02"></a>
#### MR-02 · P1 · Consume the existing transient-authority classification in the control pump
**Class:** S. **Owner:** worker lifecycle.

**Root cause/evidence:** `native-client.mjs` now attaches the existing authority-unavailable marker only to a genuine unanswered dependency/transport. `activity-lifecycle.mjs` still decides using the string code alone. Its own comment documents the old conflation: DEADLINE can be skipped for the run lifetime; all NATIVE_UNAVAILABLE errors are skipped twice then fatal. Thus a known transient outage and a definitive refusal follow the same count path. [apps/runtime/native-client.mjs:21–32](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/native-client.mjs#L21-L32), [apps/runtime/native-client.mjs:65–80](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/native-client.mjs#L65-L80), [apps/worker/conversation/continuation/activity-lifecycle.mjs:14–20](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/continuation/activity-lifecycle.mjs#L14-L20), [apps/worker/conversation/continuation/activity-lifecycle.mjs:54–73](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/continuation/activity-lifecycle.mjs#L54-L73)

**Bounded work:** use the existing authority-availability predicate in the unfenced read classification; preserve canonical diagnostics and cause. Definitive authorization/refusal/malformed-protocol errors should fail immediately. A proven nonanswer may use the existing bounded backoff inside the same absolute run lifetime. Do not change claim, ACK, capture, fenced-read or held-publication behavior; these are effect/hold barriers, not skippable polls. Agree any allowance change with the P1 table owner, rather than creating a second timeout policy.

**Tests:** update `lifecycle-activity.test.mjs` cases currently asserting a third generic unavailable ends the run. Add explicit marked transient vs unmarked refusal; several missed reads followed by steer; a refusal after two misses; deadline during backoff; revoked task lease; cancellation before initialization; fenced read after publication refusal; no repeated command claim or UUID. Existing tests at lines73–109 prove the old behavior, not the new classification. [apps/worker/conversation/test/lifecycle-activity.test.mjs:73–109](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/test/lifecycle-activity.test.mjs#L73-L109)
**Acceptance:** no definitive refusal is masked; no benign marked outage kills solely by generic-code count; original absolute deadlines and all irreversible-control uncertainty barriers remain intact.

<a id="mr-03"></a>
#### MR-03 · P1 · Define and wire foreground routine management after the originating chat ends
**Class:** S dependency, D supported product behavior, U live consequence. **Owner:** runtime routines; FE consumes.

**Root cause:** all attended routine context resolution is tied to a currently active Meri execution allocation. The selected run must be starting/running/waiting-for-approval; its allocation must be ADMITTED, unexpired and match the current render's versions and allocation hash. [apps/runtime/native-authority/routines-owner-composition.mjs:45–87](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/native-authority/routines-owner-composition.mjs#L45-L87) Routine list and reconcile also resolve/authorize through this owner. [apps/control/schedules/application.mjs:115–126](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/application.mjs#L115-L126)

This is appropriate evidence for execution capability but is not by itself a durable routine-management authority. A standalone Routines page after a completed/retired chat, a fresh sign-in, or an image change can have an existing owned schedule but no qualifying live chat allocation. Source therefore does not establish that the user can list, reconcile, narrow or cancel that routine then.

**Bounded work**
1. Add composed tests for list, detail-equivalent list filtering, prepare pause/delete, approval, read-consent refresh and cancellation with no active chat, terminal latest run, expired allocation and changed image.
2. Decide the supported contract explicitly. Recommended: keep execution/new allocation admission unchanged, but resolve read-only routine ownership and narrowing/stop management from the trusted current identity, membership, persisted schedule owner and exact schedule generation, not a running model allocation. Any ability to create/widen execution still requires the reviewed template/budget/current policy plus explicit approval.
3. Split only the management context facets in `routines-owner-composition.mjs`, `routines.mjs`, `schedules/runtime-context.mjs` and `schedules/application.mjs`; retain the native firing grant and per-occurrence admission owners. Never allow body-provided run/client/template selectors to establish authority.
4. If independent management is intentionally unsupported in the initial product, expose that exact held capability and an actionable authorized recovery route; do not require the user to start paid model work merely to stop a routine.

**Dependencies:** FE routine integration, Core current membership/owner checks, MR-01 for old original cleanup. Existing product-routines-flow tests exercise scripted authority and injected owners, not a live no-active-chat case. [apps/runtime/test/product-routines-flow.test.mjs:22–44](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/test/product-routines-flow.test.mjs#L22-L44)
**Acceptance:** a schedule remains manageable under its owner after chat completion/restart; stale/foreign generation and unauthorized widening are refused; routine “future firings stopped” and “current work physically retired” remain separate states.

<a id="mr-04"></a>
#### MR-04 · P1 · Make routine read-consent receipt repair durable across dual-owner ACK loss
**Class:** S. **Owner:** routines/read-delegation integration, with Core owner dependency.

**Root cause/evidence**
- Consent is created/resumed/narrowed before the schedule mutation. Its reference is recorded beside the schedule only after an acknowledged mutation. [apps/control/schedules/read-consent-lifecycle.mjs:30–63](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/read-consent-lifecycle.mjs#L30-L63), [apps/control/schedules/application.mjs:95–113](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/application.mjs#L95-L113)
- An acknowledged approval replay returns before repairing that consent projection. The existing test explicitly asserts acknowledged replay touches no consent. [apps/control/schedules/application.mjs:85–89](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/application.mjs#L85-L89), [apps/control/schedules/test/read-consent.test.mjs:25–43](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/test/read-consent.test.mjs#L25-L43)
- Narrow/abandon failures are logged and not durably queued here; acknowledged-not-released consent recording is best-effort. A process loss after the schedule effect but before local consent custody can leave a valid routine whose local occurrence read join refuses. The join correctly requires a matching current active consent reference, and must stay fail-closed. [apps/control/schedules/occurrence-read-consent.mjs:28–45](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/occurrence-read-consent.mjs#L28-L45)

**Bounded work:** persist a narrowly scoped consent-transition intent/reference with the immutable schedule intent before dispatch; after ACK or reconciliation, repair only the same known consent and generation. Read back Core's current consent when outcome is uncertain; if no exact readback/idempotency contract exists, add that bounded owner protocol first. Do not mint a replacement widening consent during blind replay. Persist narrowing/abandon debt and its proven disposition rather than relying solely on logs. Expose “routine active, read consent unresolved/expired” distinctly from successful unattended reads.

**Files:** `read-consent-lifecycle.mjs`, `application.mjs`, `intent-reconciliation.mjs`, `postgres-schedule-owner.mjs` and a minimal additive migration if no existing durable slot suffices; corresponding routine-consents/Core wire adapter only for exact readback. The existing log events remain observability, not authority.
**Tests:** consent created then schedule NOT_DISPATCHED; schedule ACK then local record failure/crash; withheld ACK; ambiguous schedule effect; failed pause/delete narrowing; replay after restart; consent expiry and live-login renewal; changed projection; concurrent generation; foreign consent substitution. No automatic consent widening or duplicate schedule creation.
**Acceptance:** replay/reconciliation can restore the exact acknowledged consent receipt without redispatching a schedule or granting new scope; otherwise uncertainty remains visible and firing/reads stay closed.

<a id="mr-05"></a>
#### MR-05 · P1 · Compose complete retention suppression, purge and restore ownership
**Class:** S missing production composition, D policy/authorization. **Owner:** runtime retention; memory/artifact owner supplies its class-specific bindings.

**Existing safety:** retention startup closes serving until authoritative ledger reconciliation and a read-back recovered watermark. Purge code includes shared references, legal/object locks, UNKNOWN liabilities, unpublished completion checkpoints, external deletion blocks, exact-object-version deletion and row preflight. These primitives are not missing. [apps/control/retention/startup.mjs:1–32](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/retention/startup.mjs#L1-L32), [apps/control/retention/purge.mjs:18–70](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/retention/purge.mjs#L18-L70), [apps/control/retention/purge.mjs:76–138](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/retention/purge.mjs#L76-L138)

**Actual gap:** runtime `retentionGate` explicitly constructs `purge:{enabled:false}` and states that control has no deletion owner. It also does not pass the startup's optional suppression ownership/authorization/cancellation ports. This is a safe hold, not a functioning end-to-end delete lifecycle. [apps/runtime/control.mjs:70–79](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/control.mjs#L70-L79)

**Bounded work**
1. Define the approved suppression/release/purge policy and actor authority; map product “archive/hide/delete” semantics separately. No deletion enabled by this plan.
2. Bind durable authoritative ledger, watermark, ownership, cancellation and storage/row/facts/key ports through existing startup. Prove every product read, signed download, stream, checkpoint restore and unattended occurrence observes suppression before release; cleanup Stop must remain available without content release.
3. Inventory all data classes from the single registry. Memory/artifact work owns external proposal deletion, upload custody and tombstones. Runtime owns native journals, events, SDK mirrors, completion/next-run rows and identifier-only financial evidence. Schedules currently use an unsupported retention subject and need an explicit separate cancellation/retention decision, not accidental inclusion under a conversation purge. [apps/control/retention/data-classes.mjs:21–38](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/retention/data-classes.mjs#L21-L38)
4. Preserve content-free evidence necessary for UNKNOWN liability and original retirement. Never purge proof needed to distinguish allocated/foreclosed/retired.
5. Restore behind the serving fence: restore authoritative ledger separately from stale projections, replay suppressions and in-progress purge records, verify object/version absence and row state, recover exact watermark, then permit serving. Parent owns backup deployment/runbook and migration gate; this ticket owns semantic prerequisites and acceptance.

**Acceptance:** suppression immediately blocks all content surfaces and new work while allowing authorized cleanup; a stale backup cannot resurrect suppressed content; no purge is EXECUTED with unresolved liability, unowned external data, unpublished completion, shared live reference or unknown object deletion. Destructive execution requires a later authorized rollout and real owner ports.

<a id="mr-06"></a>
#### MR-06 · P1 · Close durable lifecycle outcome readback and recovery after process/request loss
**Class:** S limited recovery surface; Q broader durability; U old-source-session replay behavior.

**Existing behavior:** durable controls are claimed once; PENDING can dispatch, DISPATCHING/UNKNOWN cannot be automatically resent; steer becomes APPLIED only after the exact SDK user-message UUID. Completion has immutable PUBLISHED readback, and Stop has its own durable fence readback. These are intentional barriers. [apps/control/conversations/lifecycle/service.mjs:53–103](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/conversations/lifecycle/service.mjs#L53-L103), [apps/worker/conversation/continuation/activity-lifecycle.mjs:83–108](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/continuation/activity-lifecycle.mjs#L83-L108)

**Missing closure:** the inspected public lifecycle family exposes control and checkpoint inspection, not a separate content-free control-outcome readback tied to a committed source-to-target transition. Replaying an exact command returns the journal receipt only if the ordinary source operation can still authenticate. That does not prove receipt recovery after a resume advances the current run, a process restarts, or source credential expires. Existing `readApplied` is a within-request recovery path using transition authority. [apps/control/conversations/lifecycle/service.mjs:64–67](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/conversations/lifecycle/service.mjs#L64-L67), [packages/contracts/conversation-lifecycle/protocol.mjs:1–4](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/packages/contracts/conversation-lifecycle/protocol.mjs#L1-L4)

**Bounded work:** first test actual composed old/new-session replay across a successful transition and lost ACK. If current source already supports it through another current-authority route, publish that exact contract and consumer instead of adding an endpoint. Otherwise provide a read-only receipt lookup under fresh current identity plus exact persisted transition custody, returning only source/target/control state and receipt IDs. Reconcile only from existing authoritative proof; an unobserved SDK message remains UNKNOWN. Do not infer APPLIED from a final assistant answer, transcript text, timeout or process death. Define owner/operator escalation for irreducible UNKNOWN without clearing the barrier.

**Files:** lifecycle service/journal transition readback, identity transition resolver, contract metadata/client and FE consumer only if required; `completion-journal` and `session-owner` receive boundary tests, not a second state machine.
**Acceptance:** ACK-loss receipt recovery never resends SDK input, reallocates a target, replays mutation or captures another checkpoint. Expired/foreign source selectors cannot release content. The user can distinguish pending, applied, unknown, source-retirement pending and result-not-released.

<a id="mr-07"></a>
#### MR-07 · P1 · Publish a precise checkpoint/version and dormant-original continuity contract
**Class:** D + Q, not permission to relax current refusal. **Owner:** runtime compatibility, release/adoption gate, FE messaging.

**Evidence:** SDK and CLI are pinned; the original admission remains tied to its admitting render. Completed-conversation next turns survive image-only changes but refuse SDK/CLI changes; pre-existing successor reservations adopt the accepting image only when compatibility holds. [apps/worker/conversation/options.mjs:20–33](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/options.mjs#L20-L33), [apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs:71–104](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs#L71-L104)

**Bounded work**
- Document separate states: admitted but unrun original; running original; retired completed turn; failed/cancelled turn with older completed checkpoint; reserved successor; pause/resume/fork checkpoint; SDK mirror append uncertainty.
- For each, specify image-only update, SDK/CLI/opaque-format update, policy/client change, rollback and expiry outcomes. Preserve current denial on incompatible SDK/format.
- Decide supported remediation: retained compatible runtime / explicit migration owner / explicit new conversation with clearly disclosed lack of session continuation. PR178 approved configuration adoption remains its own decision and must not silently become checkpoint migration.
- Add typed user-visible reasons for incompatible continuation vs transient service failure, without exposing private configuration or claiming a new conversation resumes the old SDK session.
