<a id="mr-08"></a>
#### MR-08 · P1 · Qualify the complete admission-to-next-turn lifecycle, not only happy-path endpoints
**Class:** Q. **Owner:** runtime supplies the semantic cases; release coordinator owns integrated test/release execution.

**Bounded work:** turn §4 into a composed regression suite at the exact release commit and packed contracts. Retain existing unit/protocol tests and add missing crossings of process boundaries, real transaction ACK loss, identity renewal, native currentness and provider terminal evidence. No parallel workflow/model loop.

Minimum crossings:
- initial input allocation/admission/submit duplicates and ACK loss before/after run acceptance and Temporal start;
- kickoff claim/lease expiry, task replacement, queued cancellation before allocation and durable foreclosure;
- load/currentness outage, SDK init failure, mirror append ACK loss, restored transcript/session key mismatch;
- stream disconnect/reconnect with strict cursor, duplicate event ID, late output after Stop/hold/suppression;
- terminal usage followed by capture/publication loss; completion observed but physical task not yet retired;
- Stop repeated after caller disconnect, control restart, Temporal NOT_FOUND and reconciliation exhaustion;
- next turn after completed/failed/cancelled source, with and without an older completed checkpoint, reserved before deploy and accepted after;
- steer/pause/approval wait/capture/resume/fork including retained original during fork and fresh target session;
- process-local session renewal after bearer expiry/control restart, same exact binding, with no implicit next-turn reservation or Stop.

**Evidence basis:** existing semantic tests cover many individual boundaries, including terminal events, hold failure, lifecycle commands and image continuity, but use scripted/native test owners. [apps/worker/conversation/test/terminal-settlement.test.mjs:1–22](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/test/terminal-settlement.test.mjs#L1-L22), [apps/worker/conversation/test/completion-activity.test.mjs:1–15](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/test/completion-activity.test.mjs#L1-L15), [apps/worker/conversation/test/lifecycle-activity.test.mjs:36–48](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/test/lifecycle-activity.test.mjs#L36-L48), [apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs:22–49](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/test/conversation-survives-deploy.test.mjs#L22-L49)

**Acceptance evidence:** each case records actual source/target bindings, exact image/SDK/CLI/contracts, request/control/event identity, journal transitions, allocation/task/grant disposition, checkpoint lineage and sanitized error/outcome. Require absence assertions for duplicated provider calls or unintended actions. A mock pass cannot be labeled physical stop, real SQL durability, deployed SDK compatibility or live currentness.

<a id="mr-09"></a>
#### MR-09 · P1 · Qualify provider terminal accounting and unresolved-liability recovery
**Class:** Q + operational closure, not a new budget implementation. **Owner:** proxy/accounting runtime; P1/P2 retain their existing ownership.

**Existing contract:** the Bedrock native parser accepts the supported model/stop/usage envelope, excludes unsupported cache/context-edit/tier shapes and requires a terminal message-stop/EOF. The reservation CountTokens basis is not required to equal billed input; exceeding liability holds/quarantines rather than refunding. [apps/proxy/qualification/runtime/native_usage.py:1–265](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/proxy/qualification/runtime/native_usage.py#L1-L265), [apps/proxy/product/response_settlement.py:1–296](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/proxy/product/response_settlement.py#L1-L296)

**Bounded work**
1. Freeze supported model alias/API shape, allowed stop reasons, usage fields and treatment of optional Bedrock metrics in release evidence. Current source behavior, not an old PR title, is authoritative. Any expansion of thinking, cache, context edits or provider features is a separately reviewed capability change.
2. Add terminal evidence cases: partial frame, malformed/nonterminal EOF, message_stop without EOF, disconnect after provider completion before settlement ACK, duplicated terminal, usage mismatch, output above reservation, late cancel, zero reported usage and owner replacement.
3. Inventory durable request/hold/dispatch/native evidence/settlement receipts after proxy death. Wire an exact content-free outcome inspection/reconciliation procedure using the current settlement owner's readOutcome. If proof is unavailable, retain UNKNOWN; no second inference and no automatic refund.
4. Ensure UNKNOWN liability blocks capacity reuse and retention purge until authoritative settlement/no-dispatch proof exists. Operator evidence must retain original task/grant/pricing/request binding, not use current pricing or logs as substitute authority.

**Dependencies:** active P1 budget table, P2 pair/currentness, MR-05 retention and release telemetry/runbook. No duplicate P1 timing edit, no P2 replacement implementation.
**Acceptance:** lost ACK can read back an exact committed settlement; no outcome evidence never becomes free capacity; actual usage cannot be attributed to a later run or different price version; downstream completion does not imply cost settlement.

<a id="mr-10"></a>
#### MR-10 · P1 · Qualify routine firing, overlap, cancellation and inbox continuity as one lifecycle
**Class:** Q; MR-03/04 are the concrete source gaps. **Owner:** routines runtime; Core/FE/release counterparts.

**Already present:** deterministic occurrence identities; original-owner current grants before admission/start; pause/delete/generation fences; exact admitted run and request reuse; UNKNOWN overlap barrier; paused/approval-wait same-work occupancy; explicit human stop proof bound to selected lineage; and inbox currentness for original owner with current membership. [apps/runtime/native-authority/routines-firing-authority.mjs:71–124](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/native-authority/routines-firing-authority.mjs#L71-L124), [apps/control/schedules/scheduled-job-lifecycle.mjs:29–53](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/scheduled-job-lifecycle.mjs#L29-L53), [apps/control/schedules/scheduled-run-cancellation.mjs:47–75](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/scheduled-run-cancellation.mjs#L47-L75), [apps/control/schedules/scheduled-conversation-currentness.mjs:15–53](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/schedules/scheduled-conversation-currentness.mjs#L15-L53)

**Bounded acceptance suite**
- Approve under live consent; fire without an attended browser; fail safely when consent expires, membership changes, policy revokes, schedule pauses/deletes, or Core does not answer. No attended-token fallback.
- Re-deliver one firing/restart schedule worker and control at each point: immutable occurrence claim, admission, Temporal start ACK, first output, terminal, retirement and overlap release. Exactly one conversation/run/request/event identity.
- A paused/resumable occurrence still occupies overlap; fork is independent work and must not accidentally release or inherit the occurrence slot. Unknown dispatch and unknown stop preserve the slot.
- Future-only pause/delete never claims current work stopped. Explicit current-work cancellation uses exact approved lineage and proves each original retired; concurrent resume or changed lineage cannot widen the approved selection.
- Result reaches the existing history/inbox with original-owner currentness, including partial output and late completion; foreign/archived/revoked access is withheld. Test list/transcript pagination and pool concurrency together, not only direct origin lookup.
- Reconcile after a wrapper's timeout or worker shutdown; no new firing replaces unresolved work. Old-image occurrence cleanup exercises MR-01.
- Confirm exact control/schedule-worker workflow target, grants, native operations, schema and deployment image. These are release inputs, not values to invent here.

**Files:** existing scheduled-dispatch/job/lineage/read-consent/currentness tests plus runtime product-routines-flow tests, not new duplicate scheduler. Parent owns service deployment and actual Temporal/ECS qualification. tools/domain owner owns conversational schedule preparation/list-tool bridge, not this firing lifecycle.

### 3. MR-01 stop-only reproduction and acceptance matrix

This is a planned reproduction matrix, **not executed results**. Recreate the same durable rows with fresh process-local registries, not an in-memory cached controller.

| Original state under A | Change before restart B | Current source observation / expected test | Required closure |
|---|---|---|---|
| ADMITTED/ALLOCATED running dynamic first turn | Same config, same credentials | Existing restart-retirement tests cover variants with scripted owners | Existing exact stop behavior remains |
| Running original | Image/task definition/config SHA change | Registry initial check still pins current configuration; source-confirmed refusal path | Historical stop-only resolver reaches canonical stop |
| Failed/cancelled terminal but unretired | Image/config change | Terminal run fence does not remove registry config pin | Stop/reconcile retires exact old task, then grants revoked |
| Completed and checkpointed but unretired | Image/config change | Candidate11 retires before B, so does not cover this | Retain completion/checkpoint; retire without recapture |
| Original still owned | Control capability rotated, semantic config hash unchanged | Current control digest participates in rebuilt original facts | Authenticate current caller; prove stored original ownership without treating current digest as original |
| Original still owned | Expiry, schedule-window or policy change | cleanup skips only selected expiry/state checks, not all current-render comparisons | Expired execution stays forbidden; authorized historical cleanup remains possible |
| No allocation authority ever acquired | Restart/config change | Requires durable FORECLOSED/no-authority evidence, not missing row | No task allocation, no model call; settlement only from exact proof |
| Already RETIRED and grants revoked | Image change | Existing settled historical allocation path present | Idempotent stop and no double revoke/capture/admission |
| Any old original | Tampered issuance/request/grant/task ARN/tenant/fence | Must refuse | Zero stop of unrelated resource, zero allocation |
| Later-run source vs first-turn original | Same change | Different registry construction paths; audit separately | Both use their own immutable custody; no accidental broadening |
| Reconciler timeout/stop ACK loss | Restart again | Uncertainty is a durable barrier | Next one-shot read/stop reentry settles same identity only |
| Source now cleanup-compatible but SDK-incompatible | SDK/format change | Current execution refusal is intentional | Cleanup works, next turn remains refused |

Required counters in every test: model dispatches, issuance/grant creates, allocations, native stops, grant revokes, checkpoint captures, target reservations. Stop-only must never increment the first three or checkpoint captures.

### 5. Sequencing, non-duplication and release evidence

1. **First:** reproduce MR-01 in source-level controlled tests and close historical stop-only custody. In parallel resolve MR-02 classification and MR-03/04 routine management/consent decisions.
2. **Then:** define MR-06 exact receipt readback and MR-07 compatibility behavior. These precede broad FE claims about recovery, “resumed”, “stopped” or “routine ready”.
3. **Integrate:** execute MR-08/09/10 semantic matrices using the one approved P1 timing table and the reviewed P2 pair/currentness implementation. No new permission to run is conferred by this document.
4. **Retention:** keep purge disabled until MR-05 and all class owners, external deletion, policy and restore proofs are approved. Suppression coverage and cleanup availability can be qualified before destructive purge.
5. **Release:** the release coordinator verifies migrations, exact commits/images/generated contracts, immutable runtime settings and real service wiring, reviews stale PR text, separates baseline failures from regressions and performs live qualification only when authorized.

#### Existing lane handoffs, not new implementations

- **P1:** owns all nested operation, startup, request, lease, SDK store, capture and cancellation budget arithmetic. MR-02/08 submit semantic cases to that lane. Do not maintain another table.
- **P2 PR196:** owns service-pair identity/currentness, lease replacement, security review, Docker/pinned image and live gates. The inspected PR reports focused 315 Node/453 Python checks with 21 skips and 34 mutation kills but explicitly leaves the broad gate non-green and image/live work not run. Treat these as reported PR evidence only, not results from this audit.
- **P0/PR195:** merged telemetry is evidence instrumentation, not a substitute for semantics. Parent owns generic observability, runbooks and release evidence.
- **PR178:** explicit configuration-adoption decision; not automatically a fix for old-task cleanup or SDK checkpoint migration.
- **PR197:** held supplement can receive appropriate regressions; do not relabel as runtime candidate or P2.
- **frontend owner:** public lifecycle consumer/proxies; real session/reconnect UI; Stop vs retirement state; typed incompatibility; routine/inbox display. Public lifecycle export is already present.
- **tools/domain owner:** approved task mutation and conversational schedule bridge, not routine firing.
- **Memory/artifact owner:** selected-output durable lineage, uploaded-object custody, memory/external deletion and tombstones, not global retention recovery.

#### Unresolved facts that must stay explicit

- Whether the exact Fable deployment currently runs main, candidate11, an older image or a different rendered configuration.
- Whether historical issuance custody contains all nonsecret facts needed for MR-01 without a schema change.
- Whether a real cross-render Stop has failed or an old task remains allocated. No such incident is proven here.
- Whether another current-authority API already provides cross-transition receipt readback sufficient for MR-06.
- Which runtime retention settings, ledger sink, backup/restore procedure, schedule-worker service, grant configuration and pair-currentness implementation are actually enabled.
- Whether provider/owner UNKNOWN receipts survive every real process/SQL failure boundary; unit source alone cannot establish it.
- Whether foreground routine management is intentionally limited to a live chat allocation or should be independently available. Its current dependency is source-confirmed; the supported product choice is not inferred.
- No current fresh pass count, benchmark, deployment success, physical-stop result or provider call is claimed.

## Tool and skill inventory, contracts and domain actions

Read-only source audit, 21 October 2026. Main Meri `bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996`; supplemental PR197 now `ef3fbbd964ff4ff86cc12026458fce2c88a7d787` (fresh 17:47:49 UTC; prior audited ancestor `cf634d4f70376c12ee6dd7b97ec22981dbd4cb2a`); Core default backend-import `309af848f1aa403f6c8e54aa0d22990d4230f063`; Core104 `5bf901173e839c0145069ec42e16dcb40c373ba6` freshly verified open. No implementation, test execution, runtime activation, provider request, mail action or deployment was performed. Existing tests/handoffs are source evidence only.

### Scope clarification: full original-plan completion

The user subsequently clarified that every original planned feature must be accounted for. Current source exclusions are implementation/activation status, not removal from the completion plan. All14 original skills are full-completion requirements; their15 exact asset versions remain distinct. The original-requirement crosswalk and MO/MB tickets extend this audit to deferred domain effects, procedures, Cowork, drafting, browser/device and other original features. A bounded first release may remain narrower, but cannot be labeled full Meri completion.

### 1. Executive findings and counting rules

1. There are **97 exact versioned descriptors**, not 97 currently callable tools: 86 reads (including the unmounted schedule-list contract) and 11 non-read capabilities. The legacy mapping contains 139 originals, of which 96 are selected and 43 explicitly deferred; descriptor count differs because replacements/versions are not the legacy list. Existing aliases are not missing features.
2. Main exposes a **constructible universe of 76 reads in 42 families**. #197 supplies a universe of **81 reads in 45 families**, adding research-source search, BD evidence, legal reference, LegiWatch news and Outreach to policy discovery. Neither count establishes a mounted release profile, per-run authorization or live availability. Main also has adapters that the selected startup cannot compose (Research Portfolio/Florida/Monday/task query); #197 contains receiving source for those.
3. **Mail remains uncomposed even after #197.** A conditional port exists, but it requires authoritative mailbox scopes and a durable mailbox/folder/continuation owner that production composition does not supply. Do not mark ML-22 closed merely by receiving the PR.
4. **Current Core already implements the FedRAMP locale, rule-status clocks and SAM generation/status/health observations.** Older #197 handoffs describe these as missing because they inspected an earlier Core. The current missing work is Meri adapter/context/registration, reviewed Core delegation admission and end-to-end qualification, not rebuilding those producers.
5. **Only task.create is an approvable command in the selected conversation-intent approval owner.** task.update and routines have source seams but are not equivalent executable proposals. Memory and attachment filing remain explicitly unavailable. A prepared draft or recorded approval is not an executed external effect.
6. **14 named skills have 15 pinned versioned assets, not 14 executable selected workflows.** Selected admission/runtime admit only relationship_brief and commitments_audit; other private recipes/plugin assets are not a current selected installation. Browser tools and authenticated-site workflows also remain intentionally held. Built-in TaskCreate/TaskList names in a defensive SDK universe are not Capiro task tools.

### 2. The actual end-to-end path

#### Read discovery, argument validation and result release

- Preservation catalog → exact operation binding → qualified measured execution installation → effective platform ∧ tenant ∧ role policy and activeReadOperations → current actor/tenant discovery grant → required client/source scopes → selected-skill narrowing/exclusion → `mcp__meri_reads__<id with dots replaced by underscores>`. Legacy aliases are never exposed as alternate tool names.
- The SDK tool accepts an `arguments` object and a reserved `_meriInvocation` correlation field. The hook, not the model, supplies the one-time correlation. Input schemas are fetched by exact descriptor hash, references inlined, annotation defaults removed, and advertised only when the conversion is demonstrably equal/narrower. The gateway revalidates and normalizes; model-provided authority is rejected. Every row below identifies its canonical input/output definitions and current field limits.
- General reads in profile mode mount only `activeReadOperations`; absent means no reads. A mounted tool can correctly return UNAVAILABLE on a particular run. The base `createRuntimeCatalog` requires enabled/qualified measured handlers; an installed execution view, not mutation of the preservation catalog, supplies qualification. The generic dispatch `discover()` returns [] deliberately; SDK discovery comes from the actual read owner, not this stub.
- Per call: current identity/run/subject → normalize input → current authority → durable budget reservation → recheck authority → exact bounded source call → schema/semantic output check → authority recheck → retained continuation → budget settlement → final authority recheck → release. Missing/partial source coverage never proves absence. Unknown reservation/transport/settlement retains uncertainty rather than inventing a fresh delivery.
- Core ordinary endpoint is POST `/api/clio/tools/:name`; it requires the verified service identity and native/delegated authority. Option B additionally binds exact method/path/raw-body hash, one-use token, run/attempt/fence, client/conversation selector and current release. It is read-only and does not authorize arbitrary legacy write tools. Signed retained source IDs and `actions.query` have separate fixed receiver branches. `get_client_context` is **0.3.0** while other retained reads are 0.2.0.
- Generic source limits: inert JSON admission ≤100,000 nodes/depth64/16MiB; the dispatcher caps request/response at16MiB, runtime/request/budget limits may be lower, and result bytes must fit the grant. Runtime result size is not permission to bypass narrower array/body/page limits. Advertised defaults are not SDK-inserted values: normalization and Core defaults must match exactly.

Evidence: [apps/worker/conversation/reads/tools.mjs:1-32](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/reads/tools.mjs#L1-L32), [apps/worker/conversation/reads/schema.mjs:2-9](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/reads/schema.mjs#L2-L9), [apps/worker/conversation/reads/availability.mjs:33-72](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/worker/conversation/reads/availability.mjs#L33-L72), [apps/gateway/catalog/runtime-catalog.mjs:12-35](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/gateway/catalog/runtime-catalog.mjs#L12-L35), [apps/gateway/dispatch/tool-dispatch.mjs:20-132](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/gateway/dispatch/tool-dispatch.mjs#L20-L132), [packages/contracts/retrieval-0.2/operation-contracts.mjs:1-8](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/packages/contracts/retrieval-0.2/operation-contracts.mjs#L1-L8), [apps/api/src/meri/meri.controller.ts:551-745](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-backend/blob/309af848f1aa403f6c8e54aa0d22990d4230f063/apps/api/src/meri/meri.controller.ts#L551-L745), [apps/api/src/auth/read-delegation/read-delegation.contract.ts:47-108](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-backend/blob/309af848f1aa403f6c8e54aa0d22990d4230f063/apps/api/src/auth/read-delegation/read-delegation.contract.ts#L47-L108).

#### Persistence and authority owners, not a second application model

| Surface | Canonical owner / persistence | Boundary the integration must retain |
|---|---|---|
| Tasks, action items, commitments | Core Engagement exact task/action readers; task creation effect receiver plus Meri command/intent journals | Current client wall, task-specific Portfolio grants, exact accepted effect identity, before/after authorization |
| Mail/calendar/contacts | Core Engagement stored mail and meeting readers, current mailbox/member/client authority; contact/entity owners | No direct provider read inferred from storage; separate mailbox identity from associated client; no external calendar/contact mutation tool |
| Client profile and documents | Core current client/profile/document and access-grant owners | Profile0.3 observations; document custody/readiness, current client and parent permissions; do not join by mutable client name |
| Memory/graph | Existing governed memory/passive query and graph owners | Signed page proof/helper and continuation; governance writes remain with MM/ST owners |
| Research Portfolio | Current ResearchPortfolios/Changes/review/snapshot owners | Signed retained detail/change path versus separately attended evidence GETs; current portfolio viewer grant and source revision |
| Florida / legal / news | Core authority plus Intel LegiWatch/Research owners | Exact FL/session/folder/client, stage, edition, explicit source and year; no invented implicit parser or cutoff |
| Public/federal/state data | Existing finite native Core source producers (stored Prisma sources and already-selected provider bridges) | Bound source context, mode/clock provenance, page semantics, actual source freshness and refusal/empty distinction |
| Drafts/analysis | Meri immutable artifact application and retained version owner | Draft completion/preview/filing are distinct; see MM and WS-22/23/24/PF-23 |
| Schedules | Meri routines/Temporal/schedule journals and Core policy/consent/stop authority | Not Core's legacy scheduled-task table; preparation, approval, occurrence and inbox lifecycle must stay one lineage |

#### Write preparation, approval and unknown outcomes

`meri_intents` has 11 registry rows but fewer actual offered operations. Every tool uses `{operation, arguments}` and a hook-bound delivery identity derived from tenant/actor/conversation/SDK-session/tool-use ID, excluding run/attempt so replay in the same SDK session cannot prepare twice. A fork gets a new identity. Per-run cap8; per-call timeout1–60s; an expired or revoked offer never widens mid-run. The control journal begins before preparation; lost settlement remains PENDING/UNKNOWN and never retries preparation. The output is an immutable draft or `approval_required`, never a success claim about the final business action.

`POST /api/conversations/intents/list` pages 1–50 (default20) by delivery cursor; `.../decide` binds conversation, delivery, proposal, command hash, and approved/rejected choice. Only `task.create` passes APPROVABLE_ACTIONS. Core records the decision; Meri checks the same current decision through execution, then the durable command outcome. Cross-run execution is refused. Readiness must distinguish NOT_DISPATCHED, UNKNOWN, RESULT_NOT_RELEASED and observed/executed. Same-choice retry/reconciliation is not permission for a fresh command ID. The FE pending-decision recovery issue is owned by MF, not duplicated here.

### 3. Complete 97-descriptor ledger

`*` marks a required argument. All other listed fields are optional but omission can still be refused by current delegated-client or source-slice policy. The type/limit summaries below are mechanically extracted from the pinned schemas; the linked canonical binding and schema remain authoritative for unions, nested fields, cross-field semantics and output arrays. Every output is additionally checked by its family validator, not merely JSON shape. `N` means source-compatible only, never live-qualified. Names below are the real MCP read names; intent names appear in the write table.

#### 3A. All 86 read descriptors, including the one unmounted schedule read

#### 3C. Native endpoint compatibility and source-specific invariants

- The regular native handler route maps live in `apps/gateway/sources/<family>/handler.mjs` and use the exact tool name above, not a dynamic arbitrary tool proxy. `actions.query` must use the signed alias receiver even though its canonical underlying owner is `query_action_items`; the two output contracts are different.
- Mail reads retain offset/cursor/folder/window and UTF-16 body continuation. A 50-row first page, a stored body excerpt or a partial sync cannot establish complete history or absence. Mail scopes must name current mailbox resources separately from client IDs. Provider/account/disconnect/retained-data semantics remain ML-08/09/10/13–19, not a second Meri connector project.
- Meetings and debriefs are stored/owned source reads, not full external calendar access. Preserve exact selected-client wall before every parent/source await and before release. PF-10 is the canonical authority closure for meeting readers; do not solve it only in prompts.
- `query_tasks` must retain original input/body projection and one-use Option B correlation; exact count and page both enforce Portfolio task grants. `query_action_items` is a selected-client native read, while `actions.query` is its separate complete enumeration contract.
- Research Portfolio detail/change uses retained principal and current source revision; evidence reviews perform all four current lanes and snapshots require exact record ID. An unattended delegation is not a valid ordinary Bearer for those GETs. Florida uses the Intel recipient, not Core as the data origin.
- Monday mapping is owned and checked by Core before/after the canonical read. #197 corrects an adapter expectation for a mapping receipt that Core does not emit. Do not invent an endpoint or accept model-supplied board IDs as authority.
- `search_research_sources` is six independently capped branches (clients, owned meetings, owned mail threads, owned mail messages, own Meri notes, directory notes). A selected-client filter does not itself justify tenant-wide Directory-note disclosure or unfiltered roster reads. Candidate snippets are not full decisive evidence; no source can be called complete solely because each cap returned fewer rows.
- BD evidence needs an actual owned native conversation/prospect/linked-client tuple and exact current conversation revision. Current delegated context already checks linkedClientId against the grant; do not reimplement that wall or silently replace the greenfield conversation ID with a model-selected native UUID.
- Legal reference supports parser-independent rules/versions or suitable exact-reference IDs; otherwise citation parsing is UNAVAILABLE. LegiWatch news supports exact source or personnel with explicit year and requires explicit client when folder-selected. Outreach supports explicit-client campaign list; `outreachId` detail is rejected even when empty. Core104 improves canonical Outreach visibility, but does not by itself expand these Meri slices.
- Every native time default has to be captured from the actual producer context. Fiscal year ≠ Congress ≠ session year; rule stage/comment clocks and SAM generation/status/health clocks remain distinct. FedRAMP locale must be canonical and actually consumed. Numeric/date normalization is not a license for the model to choose policy or authority.
- Public web search/scrape finite native tools are separate from browser automation. They must preserve current bounded fetch/redirect/SSRF/source-byte controls and source claims; a successful HTTP response does not make retrieved content an instruction or a verified citation.

#### 4B. Other tool names and non-promises

| Real tool/server | Exact names / mode | Source state and required closure |
|---|---|---|
| Original selected skill reads | `mcp__meri_original_reads__` + operation ID with dots replaced by underscores | Same canonical durable gateway, narrowed to selected manifest; not extra independent source operations. No alias parity bug. |
| Conversation workspace built-ins | `Read`, `Write`, `Edit`, `Glob`, `Grep` | Effective capability policy; SDK resolved-path restrictions to conversation workspace; other calls deny. A file write is not a retained/approved business artifact or a Core file. |
| Subagent | `Agent`, role names `research`, `analysis`, `artifact_production`, `verification` | Optional default-off capability, max parallel4 (default2); foreground forced, fixed inherited model, no nested Agent/Bash, bounded8/12 turns, only permitted workspace tools. Research role does not magically receive Core tools. Not an autonomous coworker/job framework. |
| Bash | `Bash` | Explicit policy + actual sandbox attestation, no unsandboxed fallback; network denied by default, networked calls require separate run-bound egress/approval owner. Source controls are not live host qualification. |
| Browser server | `mcp__meri_browser__browser_navigate`, `browser_navigate_back`, `browser_snapshot`, `browser_click`, `browser_type`, `browser_fill_form`, `browser_select_option`, `browser_press_key`, `browser_hover`, `browser_wait_for`, `browser_take_screenshot`, `browser_tabs`, `browser_handle_dialog`, `browser_close` (all prefixed) | Pinned Playwright0.0.83 server; source activation held, no actual browser server/tools while held. Interaction calls require per-call approval. No browser_eval/run_code/upload escape tools in admitted list. |
| Authenticated site workflows | No active workflows | APPROVED_SITE_WORKFLOWS=[] and SITE_WORKFLOW_ACTIVATION held; no session vault. Needs site-specific approval/terms/session/egress qualification, not merely public browsing. |
| Defensive SDK universe | TaskCreate/TaskGet/TaskUpdate/TaskList, CronCreate/Delete/List, Monitor, PushNotification, WebSearch/WebFetch, etc. | Listed to prohibit unadmitted built-ins, not mounted product promises. Do not equate these with Core task lifecycle, routines, notifications, calendars or public-native tools. |

### 5. Forty-three explicit legacy exclusions

These are recorded initial-release exclusions, not silently broken implementations and not removal from the user's full-completion plan. They remain unavailable until the mapped canonical MO/MM/MT owner qualifies them. The full-scope crosswalk records each one; aliases may consolidate only when equivalent behavior is preserved. Do not merge a historic branch to restore them wholesale. Related accepted work such as native artifact drafting, task creation or sourced memory proposals does not authorize the excluded original.

| Deferred original | Recorded boundary / disposition |
|---|---|
| `manage_research_portfolio` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `get_research_portfolio_analytics` | Portfolio analytics has domain scoring and aggregation semantics beyond source reads; fixture and scope not agreed. |
| `create_research_portfolio_snapshot` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `request_research_portfolio_output` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `save_research_portfolio_output_to_client` | Portfolio-specific filing workflow is not selected; narrow verified-artifact filing is a separate proposed command. |
| `watch_research_portfolio` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `update_research_portfolio_change_state` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `get_research_portfolio_bookmarks` | Portfolio bookmark workflow is not required by defined v1; private-source access still needs an inventory decision. |
| `create_research_portfolio_bookmark` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `get_research_portfolio_fit` | Portfolio fit scoring is a specialized business workflow; no agreed behavior or benchmark. |
| `get_research_portfolio_reviews` | Portfolio review workflow parity is not agreed; relevant records may be inventoried separately. |
| `add_research_portfolio_review_note` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `get_research_portfolio_rules` | Portfolio rule-management surface is not part of the initial workspace or command set. |
| `get_research_portfolio_imports` | Portfolio import administration is separate scope; no import workflow selected. |
| `get_research_portfolio_operations` | Portfolio operations diagnostics is a specialized workflow with unqualified permissions. |

| `find_path_to` | Relationship-path recommendation behavior and policy fixture are not sufficiently defined; graph record reads remain proposed. |
| `get_legiwatch_review` | Saved LegiWatch review workflow and its state semantics require separate acceptance. |
| `compare_florida_budget_amounts` | Florida-specific budget comparison workflow needs explicit units, provenance and expected-value fixtures. |
| `prepare_legiwatch_briefing` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `compare_legislative_versions` | Legislative version comparison behavior and legal-reference fidelity need separate qualification. |
| `save_legislative_research` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `preview_legiwatch_relevance` | LegiWatch relevance scoring is a specialized proposal workflow outside the initial commands. |
| `save_legiwatch_relevance` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `apply_legiwatch_suggestion` | Specialized Research Portfolio or LegiWatch source workflow is outside initial commands; exact authorization, side effects and fixture scope require separate acceptance. |
| `create_meeting_brief` | Existing stored meeting-brief workflow is deferred; new local native brief generation is required separately. |
| `draft_policy_memo` | Existing policy-memo workflow is deferred; new local artifact drafting is required separately. |
| `save_note` | Source note mutation is not selected; local drafting and native artifacts remain required. |
| `send_email` | External send is outside the initial source command set; mailbox identity, immutable approval and delivery idempotency unqualified. |
| `reply_email` | External reply is outside initial commands; thread ownership and provider outcome reconciliation unqualified. |
| `confirm_memory` | Direct canonical memory confirmation is beyond initial sourced proposals; review/lifecycle policy must be separately qualified. |
| `correct_memory` | Direct canonical memory correction is deferred; v1 can propose a sourced correction without editing active memory. |
| `forget_memory` | Canonical revocation/deletion and legal-hold workflow is deferred; application retention/deletion remains mandatory. |
| `get_pe_buyer_workspace` | Buyer-workspace composition and specialized analysis are not defined as initial parity. |
| `create_client` | Client creation is outside initial commands; source transaction and audit invariants not qualified. |
| `update_client_profile` | Client profile mutation is outside initial commands; field/lifecycle policy not qualified. |
| `explain_opportunity_match` | Opportunity match scoring/explanation parity has no accepted rubric or independent expected values. |
| `compare_funding_opportunities` | Funding comparison workflow has unagreed scoring and explanation requirements. |
| `build_opportunity_action_plan` | Funding action-plan workflow parity is not established by opportunity source access. |
| `get_business_development_brief` | BizDev brief composition and persisted-versus-generated behavior need explicit scope and fixtures. |
| `resolve_business_development_source` | BizDev source resolution behavior may involve provider actions; precise boundary is unqualified. |
| `save_business_development_brief` | BizDev-specific saved brief workflow is outside initial commands. |

Additional domains not present as selected descriptor promises: external calendar create/update/delete/RSVP; contact create/update/delete; persistent business-coworker assignment or chat; arbitrary provider MCP installation; direct mailbox send/reply/forward; new client/profile mutation; unconstrained web research jobs; notification subscription changes; arbitrary shell automation; special `whitepaper` tool. A whitepaper can be a governed document deliverable from qualified source reads, not a newly invented tool contract. Planner/objective generation, task management views, schedules and notification inbox are product applications with separate lifecycle owners, not undiscovered aliases. See MF/runtime/MM plans and PF-06/08/24, ST-14/15/16/17, WS-22, ML-20/23/24 for product decisions and missing consumers.

### 6. Bounded closeout tickets

Priorities: P0 = safety/qualification prerequisite; P1 = required selected capability gap; P2 = explicit scope decision or expanded semantic slice. `CONFIG/QUAL` means existing source should be installed/verified, not rewritten. These are plans only. Each ticket must produce a source receipt and later, only when authorized, exact-image/live evidence. Cross-owner references retain the canonical earlier tickets rather than duplicate Portfolio, Workspace, Settings or mail-provider projects.

### 7. Execution order, completeness and limits

1. Freeze exact supported cohort and measured truth (MT-01/15), preserving intentionally held capabilities.
2. Reconcile already-written #197 adapters/composition (MT-02), then ML-22 mail scope/context (MT-03), research/BD admission (MT-04/05), existing-observation adapters (MT-06/07/08), and reviewed slices (MT-09). These can proceed in bounded parallel, but each remains unavailable until its own receipt.
3. Qualify existing task creation (MT-10); build only missing task-update and conversational routine bridges (MT-11/12). MM owns artifact/memory/filing effects and MF owns UI decision recovery.
4. Admit only fully supported selected skills (MT-13), retain execution boundaries (MT-14), and complete the operation-level parameter/output/currentness matrix (MT-16). Runtime owner supplies exact-image/production activation gates separately.

**Inventory closure:**97 descriptor rows (86 reads +11 non-reads),14 named skills /15 versioned asset rows,43 deliberate exclusions,8 distinct preparation-tool names grouped across11 non-read operations, foreground Agent/workspace/Bash and14 held browser names. The machine-readable sidecar tools-operation-ledger.json preserves full inputSchema/outputSchema references and sourceRequirements for all97 rows. The compact table cannot replace full nested schemas and semantic validators.

**What is not established:** No main/supplement was merged or changed, no tests were executed, no live deployment/configuration or real user/provider path was inspected, no provider/mail/external action was called, and no live source availability is asserted. Current source is sufficient to plan bounded changes and distinguish missing code from missing composition/qualification, but not to declare production readiness.

**Important stale-evidence corrections:** current Core already produces certification/rule/SAM observations; current runtime CLI already supplies task-create owners and permits sourceEnabled outside schedule-worker; current #197 still does not compose mail. Old comments and handoffs are historical context only when they conflict with these pinned executable sources.

#### Fresh supplemental-head reconciliation

PR197 advanced during this audit to ef3fbbd964ff4ff86cc12026458fce2c88a7d787, merging current main. Earlier cf634d4f70376c12ee6dd7b97ec22981dbd4cb2a citations identify the inspected ancestor source, not the latest PR head. The targeted new tool-source change adds a per-measurement-pass label→SHA cache: general-read installation supplies a fresh Map, and measured handlers skip only files already matched to that exact digest in that same pass. It is not a permanent hash cache, a new qualification owner or proof that81 reads are enabled. Qualify the new bytes separately: fresh pass rereads files, a different expected digest cannot reuse the cached entry, mutation/missing/symlink/oversized source fails on a new pass, and expiration/abort still prevents receipt release. Do not reuse an old measured-source receipt merely because this is an optimization. Source: [measurement L21–27](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/ef3fbbd964ff4ff86cc12026458fce2c88a7d787/apps/gateway/catalog/measured-handlers.mjs#L21-L27), [general-read pass L34–41](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/ef3fbbd964ff4ff86cc12026458fce2c88a7d787/apps/gateway/general-reads/installation.mjs#L34-L41). No fresh-head runtime/test receipt is inferred.

### 8. Result-shape and bound index

Top-level result fields and limits are mechanically extracted below. Generic retained-page fields (where present) additionally include exact operation/version, request/trace/run/subject, input hash, source references (typically≤4096), coverage/continuation, evidenceReady:false and negative-assertion restrictions. This is not a normalized success wrapper: native outputs keep their own tool markers and shapes. `typed`/`object` means the referenced schema/union supplies nested rules; it does not mean arbitrary data is accepted. Full outputSchema references and complete top-level summaries are in tools-operation-ledger.json; family semantic validators remain mandatory. No maximum shown means no bound at that specific schema node, not an exemption from response-byte/budget/source limits.

#### Additional mandatory MT-13/MM-15 acceptance from the original plan

Commitments & Deliverables keeps commitments_audit, requiresClient:true, readOnly:true and general/client scopes; even a general launch requires a selected client. Audit authorized mail/meetings/transcripts/debriefs/tasks/actions/documents/Monday with real continuation and older promises. Deduplicate promises versus decisions/questions/proposals, retain linked record IDs, evidenced owner/due date or unknown, dependencies and acceptance evidence. Exactly completed, in progress, or no record of action are audit statuses; native task status is separate. Completion needs delivery/acceptance evidence. Monday coverage is exactly checked, disconnected, unavailable, or not mapped, with partial pages explicit. No unread page proves absence. Audit performs zero task/message/memory mutations; a later requested action uses its own immutable approved operation and observed receipt. Preserve CD-01–CD-07 semantics through focused fixtures, not seven automatic paid runs. All14 launchers retain exact client/scope metadata from Remaining Plan L172–195 and every selected procedure/version is traced.

# Meri memory, artifacts and research-output wiring audit

Prepared 21 October 2026, 17:40 UTC. Read-only connected-GitHub inspection and scratch-document preparation only. No code, configuration, PR, provider, database or deployment change; no product tests or live acceptance run.

### Verdict

This is not a missing-memory-store or missing-document-generator project. Meri main already contains substantial current-authority memory recall, immutable review candidates, Core receiving/review adapters, S3/PostgreSQL artifact custody, exact-parent revisions, verified byte downloads, preview owners, congressional/research compilers and selected-output retention. The gaps are the production composition, public contracts, durable handoffs and qualification between those owners.

The most consequential open seams are:

1. Meri197's guarded conversational memory preparation cannot activate until all four retained proposal payloads, tombstone readers, schemas/grants/recovery and the exact Core approval/execution receiver are complete. The existing safety holds are correct.
2. New surface-origin memory writes lack an authoritative retention subject; unscoped personal chat and firm-wide proposal authority also are not solved by a Memory flag.
3. Main's document CLI still composes without current-authority ports. Meri197 contains the receiving fix and additional PPTX/typed-ZIP intent path; reusing it is preferable to a second writer.
4. Six-format inspect/download does not deliver six-format creation, native Office quality, PDF conversion, preview publication, a persistent Files library, uploads, filing, sharing or a full Office editor.
5. Research can truthfully finish while its file awaits separate output approval. Selected prepare/finish handles remain process-local, and the durable selected-output recipe currently admits only Relationship Brief and Commitments Audit. A recovered chat message or private compiler fixture is not a durable, approved, downloadable deliverable.

#### Canonical ownership, no duplicate projects

- WS-23 owns coordinated all-format document-access reception; MM-09 supplies Meri-specific acceptance.
- WS-24 owns the cross-application Files/upload/preview/history/share boundary; MM-10–14 describe its backend owners and exact gaps. The Meri frontend audit owns consuming controls and navigation.
- ST-07–10 own shared Memory governance, grants, Firm/My Files and notifications. MM-06 adds Meri receiving acceptance, not replacement Settings work.
- ST-11 owns the Core owner-review public contract/frontend115 slice. MM-05 is the Meri proposal and reconciliation complement.
- Shared migration/deployment packaging, global retention recovery and native image/isolation belong to the platform/runtime tickets. MM-02/07/10/18 enumerate the payload and artifact-specific prerequisites.
- General command/native approval and tool-registry work belongs to the tools/actions audit. MM-03/14 describe memory and filing-specific receivers; do not create competing approval frameworks.
- Existing Core legacy research/job routes are inspected as current source and behavior references. They are not permission to import the old agent loop or reactivate retired product paths.

Status vocabulary: MAIN = implementation exists in main source; PR = implementation exists only in the pinned receiver; GAP = missing contract/consumer/composition; HOLD = intentional safety/authority/qualification hold; LIVE? = no live evidence in this audit. P0 applies before affected activation, not a claim of a deployed exploit.

### End-to-end ownership map

#### Memory

Recall: selected read registry → task-bound gateway → signed search_memory page → distinct one-use search_memory_eligibility proof → final release deadline/retention/budget fence. Core owns current recall eligibility; Meri may not filter an ineligible result into a fabricated successful complete answer.

Conversational write: save_memory typed intent → immutable approval metadata → exact approved memory.propose command → current run/source authority and durable claim → Meri pending-candidate outbox → Core /clio/memory/review/submit → Core owner/shared review → status reconciliation → canonical active-memory/projection receipt. A command approval authorizes candidate submission, not automatic publication. Personal owner approval and shared-review approval remain distinct.

Surface write: /api/memory/proposals/propose or correct → membership and scoped principal → retention subject → immutable candidate/outbox → same Core receiver. Today the retention-subject step deliberately refuses new surface writes.

Four separate retained conversational payloads require one coordinated tombstone lifecycle: meri_memory.candidates; meri_commands.approval_metadata; meri_commands.command_journal; meri_intents.preparations. The Core received candidate is a fifth, externally owned copy; approved canonical memory has independent retention semantics.

#### Artifacts and research

Typed intent or explicit Files request → current opaque action session → Core artifact authority + Meri-local admitted run allocation/context/descriptor → immutable artifact service/accounting → exact-version object + version/CAS/publication custody → durable receipt → canonical event/history → public inspect → verified exact-byte download. Preview publication is a separate bounded write through the same current read/retention authority; native quality is separate from structural inspection.

Selected research: exact selected skill/version/window/scope → installed read-only tools and current source reads → original compiler and retained evidence → awaiting-output-approval → distinct approved output run/grant → exact source/preparation lineage bridge → artifact version → receipt/history/UI. Research completion, output approval, artifact generation, publication and filing are separate states.

### Implementation-ready tickets

<a id="mm-01"></a>
#### MM-01 — Qualify memory recall and graph as current-authority reads (P0 activation gate)

Evidence: [signed memory adapter M L11–128](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/gateway/sources/client-knowledge/memory-signed-http-adapter.mjs#L11-L128), [eligibility M L6–31](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/gateway/sources/client-knowledge/memory-eligibility.mjs#L6-L31), [Core graph C L490–529](https://github.com/neo-capiro/capiro-backend/blob/309af848f1aa403f6c8e54aa0d22990d4230f063/apps/api/src/memory/memory.controller.ts#L490-L529).

Root cause/status: Real adapters exist, but correctness depends on exact source delegation, one-use page proof and second eligibility credential, current policy, deadline and final release. A fixture or enabled registry row is not native qualification.

Bounded work/files: Reuse these adapters and existing Core memory-receiving helpers. Pin producer/consumer wire versions and source/image measurement; qualify expiry, source gates and governed lifecycle with existing selected-read deployment. Map denied, unavailable, stale, partial and empty to distinct receipts. Keep graph path recommendation unsupported unless separately accepted, rather than aliasing arbitrary graph reads to it.

Acceptance/tests: Two tenants, same tenant/different personal owner, walled client, revoked/expired grant, superseded/expired/forgotten/legal-held item, page-body/result/hash substitution, replayed helper token, eligibility expiry during release, empty page with cursor, graph inaccessible-node leakage and stale projection. Never locally remove ineligible records and claim complete coverage. Dependencies: shared delegated-source authority, current membership, Core lifecycle/projection qualification; no new memory engine.

<a id="mm-02"></a>
#### MM-02 — Close four-payload memory retention before command activation (P0)

Evidence: [M197 composition L230–263](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/cf634d4f70376c12ee6dd7b97ec22981dbd4cb2a/apps/control/memory/COMPOSITION.md#L230-L263), [M197 bind L77–159](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/cf634d4f70376c12ee6dd7b97ec22981dbd4cb2a/apps/runtime/product-ports/memory.mjs#L77-L159), [journal M197 L12–17](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/cf634d4f70376c12ee6dd7b97ec22981dbd4cb2a/apps/control/memory/postgres-journal.mjs#L12-L17).

Root cause: Candidate tombstone and metadata ports exist in PR197, but central retention does not supply the registry/target-schema/grants/recovery/policy bundle. Payload copies survive in command and intent journals; table presence does not prove new columns/triggers exist.

<a id="mm-17"></a>
#### MM-17 — Preserve source evidence through generated artifact and later access (P0/P1 integrity)

Evidence: [generic run options M L15–23, L112–118](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/runtime/artifacts/run-options.mjs#L15-L23), [selected retention M L14–36, L64–99](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/conversations/skills/retention.mjs#L14-L36), [selected output responsibility M L5–18](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/document-application/selected-output/IMPLEMENTATION.md#L5-L18).

Root cause: Source-aware compiler paths retain exact evidence, but generic document runOptions intentionally supplies evidence/assets empty. Model-written citations or an empty evidence array must not masquerade as a verified research deliverable.

Work: Define per-output distinction among user text, model-authored uncited prose and source-backed report. Feed source-backed products only through existing authorized compiler/source receipt owners with exact source IDs/versions/text ranges/read hashes, coverage/freshness and immutable preparation pins. Preserve those facts through artifact spec/version, output approval, revision, public citation projection and filing. Recheck current source access for protected reads; show withheld provenance without leaking private snippets when access is removed. No arbitrary URL or browser JSON becomes authority.

Tests: Wrong source/record/text/hash, stale source access, missing full text, misleading complete-negative result, duplicate citation, user-edit versus source-derived text, parent revision provenance, suppressed source on later inspect/download/file. Dependency: MM-15/16, canonical read/evidence and UI source panel. Do not fabricate sources to fill generic document evidence.

<a id="mm-18"></a>
#### MM-18 — Qualify native output quality separately from structural inspection (P1 release gate)

Evidence: [public read semantics M197 L15–19](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/cf634d4f70376c12ee6dd7b97ec22981dbd4cb2a/packages/contracts/document-access-application/README.md#L15-L19), [native checks M L1–10, L44–71](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/artifacts/native/checks.mjs#L1-L10), [accepted quality M L409–415, L451–453](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/docs/plans/MERI-DEPLOYMENT.md#L409-L415).

Work: Keep the adopted python-docx/XlsxWriter/openpyxl/PptxGenJS/LibreOffice stack. Qualify exact native image/tool/library/font/corpus pins and real bytes, not only JSON containers. Test DOCX paragraphs/tables/headers/sections, XLSX formulas/charts/reference totals/caches, PPTX every slide/text/image layout, PDF every page/source DOCX lineage and ZIP data/chart correctness. Retain original and revised bytes before rendering; inspect every page/slide for clipping/corruption and compare immutable parent. Preserve unsupported feature refusals; LibreOffice recalc is not universal Excel equivalence.

Acceptance: 100% benchmark native files open, formulas/reference totals correct for declared support, no critical clipping/corruption, accepted human rubric and preserved originals; native flags remain truthful. Failure/skip receipts stay visible; PR197's Windows and provenance failures need integrated follow-up, not a blanket green claim. Dependencies: MM-08–10 and shared native host qualification. Tests are to run only under later implementation/qualification authorization.

<a id="mm-19"></a>
#### MM-19 — Bound general analysis, HTML and code output contracts (P1/P2)

Evidence: [M197 document admission L8–17](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/cf634d4f70376c12ee6dd7b97ec22981dbd4cb2a/apps/control/document-application/format-admission.mjs#L8-L17), [sandbox boundary M L4–29](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/artifacts/sandbox-analysis.mjs#L4-L29), [accepted native output scope M L24–31](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/docs/CAPABILITY-SCOPE.md#L24-L31).

Root cause: Typed table ZIP is available in M197 source, but it is not arbitrary code execution. General sandbox returns inspectionStatus/publicationStatus pending and requires exact native image/manifest/receipt/current verifier. Canonical document application admits no HTML/code artifact format.

Work: Receive typed ZIP independently. For general analysis use the approved isolated native owner, exact program/input object references, output allowlist and bounded resource/current authority, then inspect and publish outputs through existing immutable custody. Decide safe HTML/code MIME/render/sandbox/download semantics and add only approved artifact schema/receiver extensions; no generic eval or executable web preview. If held for first release, retain explicit backlog/unsupported states rather than counting ZIP as completion.

Tests: Program/input scope/hash tamper, image/manifest mismatch, unexpected output name/type, oversize/missing/duplicate outputs, cancellation/process absence, external network/credentials denial, deterministic receipt and unknown replay. HTML script/network/navigation isolation and code displayed as data must be qualified if accepted. Dependencies: shared native sandbox ticket, MM-10–12/18 and public contract owner.

<a id="mm-20"></a>
#### MM-20 — Keep full Office editing as a governed follow-on, not a flag (P2 full-scope requirement)

Evidence: [editor activation M L1–20](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/artifacts/edit-sessions/activation.mjs#L1-L20), [planned editor drafts M L38](https://github.com/neo-capiro/meri-greenfield/blob/bce707763f2cbabe1a73a99aee7b7ec206fa6996/apps/control/retention/data-classes.mjs#L38).

Root cause: Edit-session/WOPI source exists but activation is hard-held. Source records full editor as accepted follow-on after initial live version; Collabora is an evaluation candidate, not a purchased/approved production service.

Work: Follow existing requirements: at least 30-file fidelity corpus, commercial embedded multi-tenant quote/spending/legal review, approved disposable per-session hosting, WOPI conformance/locks, scan and macros/external-link/egress restrictions, current withdrawal deadline, draft retention owner/schema and exact user-edit provenance into immutable revise. UI save/draft/publish/conflict/recovery/lock/expiry states must use those receipts. No alternate hand-written Office editor or runtime boolean bypass.

Acceptance: Concurrent editing/lock contention, stale base/CAS, session expiry and revocation, crash/restore/private draft cleanup, publish exact immutable new version, native fidelity. Dependencies: MM-07/10/11/18, WS-24, separate authorized commercial/hosting decisions. Do not contact vendor or incur costs under this plan.

### Sequencing and bounded ownership

1. Freeze current M197/Core heads and merged FE115 source under MX-02; verify generated contract parity and the already received candidate11 resolution. No donor replay.
2. In parallel, finish MM-01 current-read acceptance and MM-02/04/07 retention/subject contracts; Core owns canonical memory and DTOs. UI can build only truthful held/empty/error states.
3. After retention and native action contracts, connect MM-03/05 and ST-07–11; validate pending/approved/active/projection distinctions end to end.
4. Receive MM-08/09 existing artifact composition and public access; separately complete MM-10/18 renderer/native quality. These are prerequisites to a useful Files/library claim.
5. WS-24/UI owner and artifact owner implement MM-11–14 in order: durable read identity/list → upload custody/prior reads → separately approved exact filing/share. Keep commands off until native receipts qualify.
6. Connect MM-15–17 selected research completion/approval/evidence into the existing runtime and artifact pipeline. Extend recipes only after generic skill install/source coverage acceptance.
7. MM-19/20 remain explicitly tracked accepted advanced work/decisions, not silently dropped from the full inventory. All activation, migration, provider and native-image acceptance occurs through the release checklist.

Suggested exclusive path ownership:
- Memory owner: apps/control/memory/**, relevant memory-only intent/approval modules, Core memory receiving and owner DTOs.
- Central retention/migration owner: shared class registry/purge/recovery, journals' schema/column guards and migration runner; coordinate memory changes before editing shared files.
- Artifact owner: apps/artifacts/**, apps/runtime/artifacts/** and document-application/**; public package owner alone changes generated contracts/metadata.
- Existing runtime owner: cli/product composition/prepared-run/selected durable lifecycle, coordinated with artifact/memory owners.
- Frontend owner: actual consumer routes and strict public packages; no private runtime imports.
- Core owners: native approval/execution, attachment registration/file destination and canonical memory policies; no duplicate source store.

### Required integrated regression matrix

- Authority: tenant/user/client/firm/personal, wall/grant/role revocation, live session and source expiry, exact current task/proposal/output selection.
- Mutation: before-dispatch refusal versus post-claim UNKNOWN; lost ACK; duplicate delivery/proposal; immutable hash mismatch; no retries that create a second effect.
- Retention: all payload copies and objects/previews/derivatives; pending versus published memory; suppression at each effect/release; tombstoned replay; restore fence; external deletion blocked; legal hold.
- Artifacts: all supported native formats, exact-parent revision/CAS, structural versus native inspection, byte hash/MIME/filename, preview claim/recovery, library list/direct-ID parity, read after original run ended without new generation.
- Research: exact skill/version/window, source coverage and negative assertions, Congressional/FY/stage/UTF-16 evidence, pending output approval, new approved output run, persisted completion/publication/receipt/history after restart, revoked protected source.
- UI/API: refresh/deep link/client switch/back/close; fixed operations/public DTOs; unavailable not empty; pending not saved; file generated not filed/shared; local fixture state never presented as durable success.
- Native qualification: same source/image/config pins, real isolated PostgreSQL roles/triggers/CAS and storage checksums/versioning/KMS/privacy, renderer/no-credential sandbox, process/resource cleanup. Historical fake/in-memory/source-only tests remain labeled.

### Unknowns and stopping conditions

- No live flag, migration, bucket, KMS/IAM, current user/grant, Core issuer, source/provider, native image or renderer access was exercised.
- Main versus M197 source reception and merge conflict resolution remain external state, not performed here.
- The exact product choices for surface-memory subjects, unscoped personal credentials, firm proposal grants, shared artifact audiences/comments, whitepaper templates/history carry-over and HTML/code publication remain contracts to approve, not assumptions to implement.
- New uploaded-file MIME/size/scan/OCR scope and native extractor were not established by a compatible greenfield public contract.
- Existing Core research/job output endpoints are not proof of a greenfield read bridge, writable filing approval or ported workflow.
- A complete claim requires source→public DTO→current authority→durable effect/receipt→history/UI→later reload/access/retention acceptance for every supported ledger row. Held/deferred rows stay open in the full plan. Nothing in this audit authorizes changing their holds.

## Cross-cutting source reception and release gates

### Cross-cutting source reception, release and qualification

This section owns cross-repository reception, configuration packaging and acceptance gates. It does not own a second runtime, tool gateway, Memory service, provider sender or test framework. Runtime semantics belong to MR; frontend consumers to MF; domain tools and actions to MT; Memory/artifact custody to MM. The earlier plan's X-01–06, WS-23/24, ST-07–11 and ML send-safety owners remain canonical for their shared systems.

**Priority / type:** P1 release coordination; current documentation inconsistency, not a missing chat implementation.

PR194's body still records October20 releases F/G/H and zero provider calls; the user separately reported that Fable obtained a chat response on October20. PR195's body retains draft/unmerged language although GitHub reports merged and its head is an ancestor of main. PR115 body quotes an older head. Historical status/ledger prose cannot override present source or the user's reported success.

**Bounded work after implementation authorization:** Maintain one reviewed, dated release register in existing shipping/coordination documentation, linking exact current repo commits, PR heads, image digests, configuration/contract/SDK/CLI versions, migration ledger and acceptance receipt. Correct generated `ship-packet.mjs` risk text to distinguish compatible image replacement, incompatible SDK/format and unfinished/in-flight work; correct its focused test expectations. Amend stale descriptions only through their existing owners. Preserve historical receipts as historical rather than rewriting them to claim a later pass.

**Acceptance:** Every public claim answers which source/image/environment/time and which exact scenario; historical first-chat success remains recorded; candidate11 source does not imply live qualification; an unknown is explicit. A generated packet cannot print “all conversations fail after every render” for an image-compatible release.

**Priority / type:** P0 activation prerequisite; implementation/consumer/contract and configuration are distinct.

<a id="mx-05"></a>

**Priority / type:** P0 release gate; existing implementation plus missing fresh native evidence.

Source/proxy/renderer/browser additions after an earlier image change its closure. EX-06 records hard-coded host Python/LibreOffice/pptxgenjs paths in some artifact tests; host success is not container inclusion.

Include only accepted owners and required packages for the selected profile. Read source and independently extract/measure the immutable image; do not rely on a matching tag.
