<a id="execution"></a>
## Ownership, sequencing, tests and definition of done

### Integrated execution plan

#### 1. Implementation authorization and first handoff

This document is **plan-only**. The next coding agent must first receive a separate implementation instruction. Until then, the permitted deliverable is this plan and its evidence, not repository edits, new branches, PR comments, merges, test execution, builds or deployments.

Once implementation is authorized, start from the exact current refs, recheck divergence and acquire the existing owners' latest completed source. Do not assign another P1 nested-budget implementation, another P2 proof-reuse effort or a second receiver branch. The user owns final merges. Fable/runtime retains shipping ownership; do not contact or direct an external agent without the user's authorization.

Before coding, each accepted ticket gets:
- One accountable source owner and one independent reviewer.
- Current base/head/tree, exact files and contract versions.
- A failing regression or a precise absent consumer/contract fixture.
- Dependencies, feature-off behavior and bounded acceptance.
- Explicit status: source work, receiver reconciliation, decision or operational qualification.
- A no-duplication pointer for shared boundaries below.

A passing source ticket may become **source-complete, activation-held**. It is not closed as a delivered user capability until its full return/recovery path passes.

#### 2. Canonical owner map and file-ownership constraints

| Boundary | Canonical owner / implementation unit | Other lanes consume, do not duplicate |
|---|---|---|
| Main + PR197 successor review | Existing Meri receiver; MX-02 | All new Meri source starts from one reconciled candidate; #189/190/191/192 donor work not reapplied |
| P1 nested startup/deadline table | Existing active P1 runtime owner; MR references | MX-09 measures results; no second timeout table or ad hoc larger constants |
| P2 proof reuse/replacement | Existing PR196 owner | Currentness/placement collision resolution and security review; PR-B follows existing PR-A gate |
| Public contracts and generated browser receivers | Meri contracts owner with FE receiver | One exact B1/B2/lifecycle DTO; shared generators/package manifest single-owned |
| FE Meri observation/session stores | Frontend Meri owner | Approvals and lifecycle controls reuse the same observer/cache/current identity, not local divergent state machines |
| Action session/current run/context authority | Meri runtime/control + Core identity owner | UI cannot choose tenant/actor or turn legacy service credentials into user authority |
| Generic intent preparation/approval/execution receipts | MT command/intent owner | Task.create remains canonical; Memory/filing/schedule commands require their own accepted domain contracts |
| Core task mutation and task reads | Core domain owner with MT adapter | MF consumes; no direct UI/provider mutation bypassing Core grant/idempotency |
| Routines/schedules/background firing | MR schedule lifecycle owner | MT owns conversational preparation/list/decision bridge; MF owns page; one occurrence/consent/cancel journal |
| Planner/objective continuation | Existing Core Planner/Strategy owner | Meri supplies bounded source/reader/run receipts only; do not restore the superseded Meri Planner loop |
| Memory canonical storage/permissions | Core Memory owner + MM custody/retention | Settings owner review and conversation proposals consume one wire/governance contract |
| Memory four-payload tombstone bundle | MM | MX-04 packages migrations/grants; no second Memory purge implementation |
| Global retention suppression/purge/restore | MR retention owner | MM artifacts/Memory register owned classes; runtime holds remain until deletion and proof owners exist |
| Artifact custody/version/current access | MM + existing Workspace artifact bridge | MF Files/document access reuses accepted public receiver; no new storage platform |
| Selected research/output workflow | MM selected-output owner | MR provides generic run/checkpoint/Stop; selected prepare/completion receipt recovery is not fixed by checkpoint continuity alone |
| Mail send safety and OAuth/provider identity | Prior ML-02–10/16 owner, Core/Engagement sender | Meri read composition and proposals are separate. No new SMTP/OAuth stack or direct send fallback |
| Portfolio/Workspace Meri entry | Prior PF-02/03 and WS-22/24 with MF/MT | One selected-client/entity/artifact contract; no label/name-based implicit authority |
| Settings Memory/preferences and account connection | Prior ST-07–14 with MM/MF; provider identity ML owner | Owner review is not all Memory governance; account OAuth status is not Meri execution permission |
| Migration/role/readiness packaging | MX-04 with service owners | Domain tickets define schema; one central order/hash/grant packet |
| Image/material/release/operator qualification | Existing shipping owner, MX-05–14 | Source lanes prepare evidence, do not independently mutate cloud/runtime |

**Collision rule:** a shared source file has one active owner per receiving batch. In particular, serialize changes to `apps/runtime/cli.mjs`, `apps/runtime/config.mjs`, `apps/runtime/product-ports/*`, `apps/control/action-host/session/owner.mjs`, `apps/worker/conversation/activity.mjs`, contract package manifests/generators, FE Meri chat/observer code, and migration/feature renderers. Parallel lanes may own separate modules/tests and submit explicit receiver patches. They may not overwrite one another's branches or guards.

#### 3. Practical staged work

##### Stage A · Freeze and reconcile, without activation
1. Recheck all default and candidate heads; record live evidence separately.
2. Review current PR197 successor already containing candidate11; preserve merged FE115 source on frontendmain24316f91, review held Core104 and exact B1/B2 parity. No duplicate FE115 reception or repeat candidate11 merge.
3. Resolve P2's currentness/placement intersections through its existing owner; consume P1's current contract without duplicating it.
4. Build the supported/held capability matrix and source/contract/schema/image dependency manifest.
5. Confirm the full original-plan crosswalk and which fixture/legacy/advanced features are held, mandatory, conditional or explicitly superseded. A UI promise can be removed or truthfully disabled only by the product owner; do not silently relabel it complete.
6. Prepare the failing fixtures for concrete approval, cleanup, selected-output, routines and retention gaps.

**Exit:** one reviewed source baseline, no duplicate donors, no changed activation flags, all parallel ownership clear.

##### Stage B · Close authority, durable receipt and recovery foundations
Run compatible work in parallel under separate owners:
- **Frontend:** fail-closed approval/history/cache behavior on definitive access loss; persist pending action identity before dispatch; reconnect/reobserve after a successful decision; retain input/draft only under matching identity and intended retry semantics.
- **Runtime:** reproduce and close stop-only cleanup of older render originals; distinguish marked transient authority nonanswers from definitive denial; retain current run/Stop/checkpoint/UNKNOWN invariants and source changes already present.
- **Commands/tools:** exact task.create effect/receipt/reconciliation; implement missing domain action authority before advertising writes; retain lost-ack/no-redispatch semantics.
- **Memory/artifacts:** complete four-payload retention/tombstone ownership, selected output durable preparation/completion and artifact authority/runtime reception.
- **QA:** paired exact-base/head source failures, current public DTO/generator compatibility and controlled negative fixtures.

**Exit:** no selected effect can become an untracked duplicate; no stale unauthorized data remains visible through an access-loss transition; old liabilities are not erased to resume work; held paths remain closed.

##### Stage C · Finish existing consumers before inventing new services
Parallel, after their contracts settle:
- Wire supported conversation lifecycle controls to the existing exported lifecycle contract.
- Complete ordinary chat composer/draft/file/preferences/model controls only for their supported public contracts.
- Consume existing artifact inspect/download/version access and real authority ports; correct unsupported format/preview display.
- Complete actual read-family composition, exact selected installation/discovery and Core delegated allowlists.
- Bridge supported task/routine intents and durable status/inbox management; a finished chat must not accidentally be the only way to manage a long-lived schedule.
- Implement Portfolio/Workspace/Settings handoff adapters on the canonical selected-client/entity/document contract.
- Route mail/calendar through their existing canonical read/provider owners; sends remain behind shared intent/idempotency/recipient/authority prerequisites.

**Exit:** each accepted visible action produces an actual durable result or truthful unavailable response and refreshes correctly after reload. New implementation is limited to verified missing consumers/contracts, not rebuilding already-inspected owners.

##### Stage D · Resolve accepted missing product contracts
Do not combine unrelated ambitions into one large rewrite:
- Attachment ingestion/read/filing needs complete custody, bounds, authority, retention and destination semantics.
- Missing catalog families need actual Meri adapters and delegated admission; existing Core FedRAMP/rule/SAM producers are reused, not rewritten.
- Memory correction/new-surface contexts need authoritative actor/client/retention and versioned public DTO.
- Broader mutations, new selected skills, whitepaper/congressional generation or unsupported analysis/document paths need explicit expected output and governed-effect contracts.
- Browser/Bash requires its existing isolation/host/network/cleanup qualification and current capability admission; leave off while unqualified.
- SDK/format configuration adoption beyond compatible image changes is an owner decision against current continuity, not automatic receipt of old PR178 patches.
- Optional UI fixture controls and intentionally excluded legacy tools remain clearly unsupported unless the owner accepts them.

**Exit:** all accepted rows have executable contracts; unresolved product choices remain named open items, not hidden estimates.

##### Stage E · Qualify the combined source and native candidate
1. Run clean locked dependency setup and complete source/regression inventories.
2. Resolve actual source/fixture failures; keep native/host limitations visible.
3. Use disposable PostgreSQL16 and narrow roles for migration/receipt/retention/contention/recovery tests.
4. Build and independently inspect the exact image only after authorization; qualify SDK/CLI/options/skills/read installation, runtime packages, PKI and selected executors.
5. Exercise service-backed FE integration against the same pinned API/contracts, not synthetic fixture endpoints.
6. Validate redaction, metrics and operator alert paths without confusing missing data or historical logs with success.

**Exit:** source/native acceptance is attached to the exact combined tree and profile. No deployment or provider action is implied.

##### Stage F · Separately approved operator release and live acceptance
Use the gate checklist below. Scope to the approved tenant, capability subset, provider/model, cost ceiling and time window. Stop on unknown outcome and observe; never blindly replay an action. Preserve old liabilities and fallback/rollback evidence. User-authorized actual mail/task/provider effects require their specific targets/content/amount/permissions, not merely permission to run a test suite.

**Exit:** current end-to-end receipts establish only the capabilities actually tested; incomplete ones remain held.

#### Full-product continuation lanes after the foundation stages

These are required full-completion lanes, not permission to stop after first-chat or a limited safe release. The earlier stages can overlap with them only after their contracts and authority dependencies settle.

| Lane / owner | Bounded work | Start dependencies / exit |
|---|---|---|
| Product interaction / frontend owner | MG-02/03 selected skill and original design traceability; MF consumers; desktop/tablet/phone behavior; natural-language selected-version revision MB-04 | Exact contracts and source scopes first; every original mapped control has real state, reload and negative tests, not a fixture link |
| Cowork/procedure owner, using existing control/SDK | MO-01/02 versioned procedures, durable goals/children, steering and safe independent work after a rejected action | MR durable continuation, MT intent authority and MM outputs; all four original Cowork traits and T5–T13 outcomes demonstrated |
| Core domain adapters | MO-04/05/06/07/08/15 governed mail, Research Portfolio, watches, funding/graph analytics, prospect/note mutations and named connectors | Canonical Core/provider owner accepts typed authority and outcome contract; no duplicate sender, Planner loop, general mutation bearer or shadow database |
| Congressional drafting owner | MO-03 exact-base deterministic amendment/workbench | Cowork traits and evidence custody first; T16–T18 include ambiguous anchor refusal, exact applied text/redline and revision-bound approval |
| Retrieval/reliability owner | MG-01/04/05/06 and MO-10/14, using existing SDK/catalog/search and indexing owners | Reconcile existing R2 receipts, then changed/new fixtures; preserve exact source semantics, dependency refresh, job cost/context/fairness; optional router/JEV adopts only if measured gate passes |
| Advanced interaction owners | MB-01/02/03/05 browser takeover, named authenticated sites, user-device bridge and source-event triggers; MM-20 full Office editor | Shared runtime/egress/custody/schedule contracts first; each capability gets its own permissions, restart/revocation, cancellation and native/live acceptance; no blanket activation |
| Release/retirement owner | MO-09/11/12/13 disable/restore, retired-code/resource cleanup and provider comparison/default disposition | Current replacement acceptance and reachability evidence first; retained records/authorizations preserved; privileged/cloud actions separately approved |

**Parallelism:** frontend, evidence/domain reads, native artifacts and deterministic Congressional engine can progress on separate owned modules after shared contracts freeze. Serialize command authority/schema changes, runtime composition, SDK profile/manifest updates, common UI observation state and final image/release. Existing P1/P2 owners remain exclusive. MO/MB/MG tickets consume existing MF/MR/MT/MM/MX boundaries rather than creating competing implementation projects.

**Full-product acceptance registry:** assign each original D/T/GF/W/REL/RG/master/remaining-plan row one canonical implementation ticket, an accountable delivery role, current source/PR state, precise acceptance case and release receipt. Attach all dependent row IDs to one ticket rather than creating duplicate work. Any subordinate original annex detail not inspected in this audit stays an explicit verification prerequisite in that registry; it is not silently assumed implemented. No full-completion sign-off until those details are reconciled and every required row passes or an explicit user-approved scope change is recorded.

#### 4. Tiered test plan

| Tier | Environment and what it proves | Required evidence | Does not prove |
|---|---|---|---|
| T0 Static inventory | Read-only current source and public contract comparisons | Route/control/catalog/call-chain/migration/profile manifests, blob parity, exact refs | Runtime behavior or deploy |
| T1 Pure/unit/contract | Locked local source with synthetic data and no providers | Input/output schemas; deny/unsupported/limits; generated public provenance; byte/hash/cursor/CAS invariants | Native DB, real SDK/model or UI-service integration |
| T2 Mounted composition | Canonical public/proxy/control/worker owners with scripted ports | UI handler→actual handler→durable fixture receipt→observer/history; auth/replay/cancel/unknown | Deployed topology, real provider latency or Office usability |
| T3 Native persistence/isolation | Disposable PG16, narrow roles, Linux/nonroot, independent pools/processes | Real transactions/locks/constraints/grants/contention/ack loss, process-group absence, restored state consistency | Product database installed state or cloud topology |
| T4 Immutable image | Exact image digest and pinned package profile | Source/archive/native dependency/SDK/CLI/skills/operation/PKI measurements, 24-case gate plus relevant executor suites | Live Core/provider permissions or user acceptance |
| T5 Service-backed integrated UI | Approved nonproduction candidate with matching FE/Core/Meri refs | Actual session/stream/history/approvals/files/tasks/routines/client isolation and failure injection | Full provider effect or production qualification |
| T6 Controlled provider/live | Explicitly approved tenant/data/provider/model/cost/effect scope | Actual current authority, minimal allowed model/read/write, durable receipt, no duplicate after uncertain acknowledgment, current UI/history | Unselected features, all tenants or future versions |
| T7 Representative task/operability | Approved real corpus and release window | Grounded correctness, usable artifacts, latency/cost, alerts, restart/deploy/restore/rollback drills | Unlimited reliability or automatic future activation |

Mock and synthetic tests must remain explicitly labelled. Any required native case skipped is an unmet gate. A narrow first-chat smoke is valuable but does not prove tool writes, mail, Memory, background work, browser isolation or full task quality.

#### 5. Minimum integrated regression scenarios

The following is a scenario inventory, not a command list to run now. Each must bind the exact source/profile/identity and record expected durable state before and after.

| ID | Scenario | Required observable result |
|---|---|---|
| E01 | Authenticated selected-client first chat | One admission/run, real response, durable transcript and correct current actor/client |
| E02 | Reload/new tab after completion | Same messages/citations/artifacts, correct ordering and no prompt redispatch |
| E03 | Double click and lost submit acknowledgment | Same request identity reconciles to one accepted effect/run; no duplicate |
| E04 | Transport disconnect before/after first text | Cursor replay neither drops nor duplicates durable events; browser close does not silently Stop |
| E05 | Session expiry and transient Core503 | Retryable unavailable differs from revoked identity; no unauthorized bytes or false success |
| E06 | Sign-out/tenant/client change with stored draft/intent | Old data/credentials/receipts cannot cross the new identity or client |
| E07 | Stop at queue/load/start/model/tool/output phases | Logical stop fence and physical cleanup/unknown status are distinguishable; no later unauthorized effect |
| E08 | Failed/cancelled turn after completed checkpoint | Next allowed turn uses last valid published checkpoint, not failed partial state |
| E09 | Image-only release after completed turn | Existing conversation and pending reserved successor continue under candidate11-compatible rules |
| E10 | Deploy/rotation while old original is in flight | Historical Stop/retirement can close old work safely; current execution checks are not weakened |
| E11 | SDK/CLI/format incompatibility | Closed refusal before persistent successor acceptance; clear recovery/new-chat choice |
| E12 | Resume/fork/retry/steer/pause | Correct exported contract semantics, exact target, current scope and one receipt; unsupported cases explicitly unavailable |
| E13 | Approval allow/reject/expiry/revoke | Captured decision persists before dispatch; exact command only; stale approval refuses |
| E14 | Approval response lost/reload/paging denial | Read-only reconciliation, no automatic repost, current-access cache invalidation |
| E15 | Approval advances waiting run | Chat observation restarts or rereads immediately; terminal/new output appears without reopening |
| E16 | Task.create commits then visibility revoked | Durable tenant-bound effect recorded once; current result access denied; no duplicate retry |
| E17 | Unsupported task mutation | UI/tool does not imply success; no fallback to raw Core mutation |
| E18 | Read active-set and revoked operation | Only measured admitted operations are discoverable/executable; revoked/missing owner refuses |
| E19 | Research/Florida/Monday/task sources | Actual delegated Core endpoint, correct selected client/pagination/time semantics and cited evidence |
| E20 | FedRAMP/rule/SAM adapter adoption | Existing Core observation fields consumed exactly; absent/not-admitted adapter remains unavailable |
| E21 | Mail list/search/thread | Correct connected account/tenant/client policy, truthful unsupported attachments and no read→send escalation |
| E22 | Mail/task provider mutation uncertainty | Existing provider/command intent owns no-resend/reconciliation; no false receipt |
| E23 | Memory recall after correction/revocation | Current revision/permission, prior content not reused incorrectly, provenance retained |
| E24 | Memory proposal approved/rejected/lost acknowledgment | Correct owner and durable outcome; four payloads retained/tombstoned coherently |
| E25 | New-surface Memory context | No fake conversation/client authority; held path stays503 until accepted contract exists |
| E26 | Attachment upload/read/file | Bytes/hash/MIME/size/scan/current custody tied to exact actor/client; rejected file never appears usable |
| E27 | Artifact generation and publication | Valid content+version+hash+evidence; publish once; current Files/chat access |
| E28 | Six-format inspect/download | DOCX/TXT/XLSX/PPTX/PDF/ZIP exact receiver/MIME; invalid/expired/foreign references refuse |
| E29 | Preview/export/native open | Real renderer/native-reader opens promised format; formulas/layout/citations correct; ZIP not disguised preview |
| E30 | Selected research output restart/lost ack | Durable prepare/completion recover without duplicate file/effect; unpublished state not labelled completed |
| E31 | Research citation contradiction/injection | Current cited evidence supports claim; source text cannot authorize tools/effects or fabricate citations |
| E32 | Routine create/update/stop/list from completed chat | Durable schedule management does not accidentally require an active foreground run; authority remains narrow |
| E33 | Routine overlap/retry/DST/timezone/worker death | Exact occurrence/idempotency/consent, no double fire or silently lost due occurrence |
| E34 | Consent revoked or Stop during routine execution | Current cancellation proof/retirement, durable unknown where required, truthful inbox status |
| E35 | Objective/Planner continuation | Core owns workflow; source/run/reader grants exact; no resurrected legacy loop |
| E36 | Retention suppression→purge→restart/restore | Suppressed data stays inaccessible, class-specific deletion receipts, no restore resurrection |
| E37 | Browser/Bash denied or unqualified | No host/network/process starts; refusal before effect |
| E38 | Approved browser/Bash isolated execution | Current host/egress policy, bounded output, no credential escape, complete cleanup/absence proof |
| E39 | Owner/proxy partner replacement / fatal retirement | Current proof reuse semantics; old generation invalidated; no request resend after timeout |
| E40 | Budget/terminal usage missing or contradictory | UNKNOWN liability preserved; no invented usage or release of reserved spend |
| E41 | P0 telemetry failure/redaction | Underlying work semantics unchanged; dropped counter/alerts truthful; no secrets/prompt/client identifiers |
| E42 | Forward rollback and schema compatibility | Previous image redeployed through new valid material/revisions; retained receipts and liabilities remain valid |
| E43 | Mixed versions/frontends/contracts | Explicit version/source refusal or accepted backward compatibility; no silently misparsed approvals/output |
| E44 | Inaccessible/empty/stale provider data | Truthful unavailable/no-results distinction, no fabricated success/evidence |
| E45 | Canonical cross-module handoff | Portfolio/Workspace/Settings context preserved with entity IDs; effect appears in receiving module after reload |

Every accepted named tool/route family also needs its table's positive, negative, pagination/bounds and currentness cases. These45 integrated scenarios are not substitutes for the finer per-ticket tests or a claim that all45 were executed.

#### 6. Operator gates and order

**Gate O0: scope/evidence freeze.** Exact FE/Core/Meri refs, selected capability profile, contracts/SDK/CLI/version compatibility, expected source/image hashes, current PR dispositions and active owners. Confirm ordinary product versus restricted preview; do not weaken preview to make demos pass.

**Gate O1: source qualification.** All affected P0 semantics resolved, relevant P1 core flows complete, receipts distinguish failures/skip/native gaps. Independent review of authority/currentness/intent/retention changes.

**Gate O2: native database packet.** Explicit approval for targeted DB/schema/grant action, backup/restore readiness, immutable ledger prefix, migration order and narrow roles. Record exactly what is installed; ordinary first-chat stages do not install held integration capabilities.

**Gate O3: image/material packet.** Authorized immutable build/push, source/archive/native measurements, vulnerability scan, SDK/profile/skills/read installation parity, credential/PKI material created or handled only through separately approved operator process. Do not publish secret values in evidence.

**Gate O4: topology/deploy packet.** Existing owner applies exact reviewed templates/material versions under one mutation operator, preserves private/public route denial and single-control assumptions, observes each ordered stage. A failed or uncertain operation is checked before retry. Forward rollback prepared in advance.

**Gate O5: source-service readback.** Exact task/image/config/source revision, healthy processes and truthful dependency readiness, Core routes/roles/features, FE native-auth/source pins. Liveness alone does not close authority.

**Gate O6: controlled end-to-end acceptance.** Minimal authorized chat/read/effect scope, cost and provider; record exact request→effect→receipt→history/UI and safety negative cases. Actual mail or external notification needs its own recipient/content/effect authorization. No old smoke receipt qualifies a changed profile.

**Gate O7: operability and user release.** Current metrics/alerts/renewal/retention/restore/Stop/rollback proofs, supported/held UI truthful, owner ratifies release scope. A limited preview and full-product launch are named differently. Existing fallback is retained unless explicitly authorized to remove or replace.

#### 7. Definition of done

For each requested capability, “wired” requires all applicable links:
1. A real mounted UI or discoverable operation, not a fixture-only screen or catalog placeholder.
2. Typed argument/result and versioned public contract, correct bounded errors and unsupported behavior.
3. Fresh tenant/actor/client/run authority through every hop; delegated/approval scope does not widen.
4. Runtime/startup owner and real downstream service/provider/persistence available for the selected profile.
5. Durable idempotent effect/output receipt, or explicit durable UNKNOWN with read-only reconciliation.
6. Cancellation/Stop/retention/revocation handling at external waits and before output release.
7. Immediate UI refresh plus history/reload/reconnect/restart behavior.
8. Relevant source/native/security/provider/format acceptance at the exact shipped source/image/config/schema.
9. Operational ownership, telemetry, expiry/renewal and tested rollback/recovery.
10. No conflicting duplicate owner, misleading success copy or unreviewed external effect.

A capability can be **source-complete, activation-held**; it cannot be called delivered. An unsupported row can be explicitly excluded from a limited release but remains incomplete in the full-product acceptance registry. Conditional original alternatives require their stated decision evidence; an original product promise is not optional merely because its source is absent. A failure is not closed merely because the same failure exists on the base. A live chat win is preserved without overstating it as full product qualification.

#### 8. Honest limits and unresolved evidence

- This was a connected-GitHub source audit with scratch documentation only. No app was operated, model/provider queried, source suite run, database inspected or migrated, image built/pulled, cloud configuration read back, external agent contacted or release performed.
- Fresh branch/PR metadata was read; source excerpts are pinned. Branches can move after this snapshot. Current private/unpublished external work beyond the supplied context was not enumerated or assumed complete.
- Whole-root recursive Git tree requests failed due to transport; smaller nontruncated subtree/contents inventories were used. Domain ledgers disclose any uninspected producer or unresolved path rather than implying all repository bytes were audited.
- GitHub compare can truncate file lists; full paginated changed-filename reads were used for the large receiver and collision census. Exact current ancestry and selected blob parity support the stated donor dispositions.
- Source-confirmed guards/gaps are distinguished from inferred consequences that require a targeted failing fixture or native reproduction. No live compromise is alleged.
- Prior source receipts are not rerun here. Windows/Linux/nonroot/PG/SDK/proxy/native renderer differences remain material; counts are not aggregated across overlapping runs.
- The current deployment's feature flags, source-image pins, real database ledger, private networking, credentials/PKI validity, provider consent and cost availability were deliberately not inferred from code or old docs.
- Representative model quality, actual Office/PDF usability, current end-to-end mail/calendar connection, exact revocation latency, concurrency/scaling and disaster recovery require the stated separately approved acceptance.
- Product decisions remain for the semantics of unsupported fixture actions, model/provider alternatives, broad mutation scope, advanced workflows and configuration adoption. Original full-product thresholds remain the baseline; a changed threshold qualifies only an explicitly named limited release unless the user approves a product-scope change. Choosing a smaller release is not completion of deferred rows.
- The plan closes known source and lifecycle gaps at implementation-ready depth; it does not claim exhaustive runtime execution or guarantee absence of every defect.
